patippuri
tamiḻukkum tamiḻarukkum vaḷamum valimiyum cērkkak
kūṭiya paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷiyellām tēṭiyeṭuttut tamiḻ kūṟum ulakiṟku vaḻaṅkum tolinōkkup pārviyuṭaṉ  tamiḻmaṇ patippakam toṭaṅkappaṭṭatu. ataṉ vāyilāka moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇariṉ nūṟṟāṇṭu niṉivāka avar eḻutiya nūlkaḷ aṉittiyum cērttu avaruṭiya maṟivukkup piṟaku 2000-ttilum, palvēṟu itaḻkaḷilum, malarkaḷilum veḷivantu, nūl vaṭivam peṟāta pāvāṇariṉ ariya kaṭṭurikaḷi ellām tokuttu 2001- lum orucēra veḷiyiṭṭu ulakeṅkum uḷḷa tamiḻarkaḷukku vaḻaṅkiṉōm.  pāvāṇar vaḻi nili aṟiñarkaḷāṉa mutumuṉivar 
irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉār avarkaḷum, maruttuvar ku.pūṅkāvaṉam avarkaḷum ivvariya kaṭṭurip putiyalkaḷ nūl vaṭivam peṟuvataṟku mikavum uṟutuṇiyāka iruntaṉar. ipperumakkaḷi naṉṟiyuṇarvuṭaṉ niṉivukūrkiṟēṉ.
collārāyccit tuṟiyil tēvanēyar oppaṟṟa taṉit
tiṟamiyuṭivar eṉṟu maṟimaliyaṭikaḷum, nāvalan tīvukku  nantamiḻē tāyeṉṟu pāvēntarum, tamiḻukkum tamiḻ
nāṭṭukkum naṟṟoṇṭu āṟṟiyavar pāvāṇar eṉṟu pēraṟiñar aṇṇā avarkaḷum, veṭṭa veṭṭak kiṭikkum taṅkac
curaṅkam pōṉṟavar eṉṟu tamiḻiṟiñar irācamāṇikkaṉārum, tamiḻakam moḻittuṟiyilē pāvāṇar pōṉṟa oru aṟiñari iṉṉum peṟṟut taravilli eṉṟu paṉmoḻip pulavar
appātturiyarum, kuṟimatiyar tēkkivitta kariyiruḷi nīkka vanta maṟimaliyār vaḻivanta niṟimaliyār pāvāṇar eṉṟu mēṉāḷ pēravittalivar tamiḻkuṭimakaṉ avarkaḷum, tamiḻar yār? etirikaḷ yār? eṉṟu āyntu aṟintu kāṭṭiyavar pāvāṇar eṉṟu pērāciriyar iḷavaracu avarkaḷum,  ōrāyiram āṇṭukaḷ ōyntu kiṭantapiṉ vārātu pōla vanta māmaṇi pāvāṇar eṉṟu mutumuṉivar iḷaṅkumaraṉār avarkaḷum kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷa  perumikaḷukkuriya pēraṟiñariṉ nūlkaḷi mīḷpatippaka veḷiyiṭuvatil perumi koḷkiṟēṉ.
pāvāṇar nūlkaḷ attaṉiyum tamiḻmoḻikku ēṟṟam urippaṉa. tamiḻi āriya iruḷiṉiṉṟu mīṭṭuk kāppaṉa. vīḻntu
paṭṭa tamiḻaṉukku viḻippūṭṭa vallaṉa. āyiramāyiram āṇṭukaḷāka aṭimippaṭṭukkiṭakkum tamiḻmoḻiyi mīṭkavallatu eṉṟu  pāvalarēṟu peruñcittiraṉār avarkaḷiṉ kūṟṟi ik kaḷañciya veḷiyīṭṭil pativu ceyvatu ematu kaṭamiyākum.
pāvāṇarit tūkkip piṭittāltāṉ tamiḻiṉam urup
paṭamuṭiyum - uyaramuṭiyum. pāvāṇar koḷkikaḷ tamiḻar uḷḷamellām nilittu niṟpataṟkum, pāvāṇar nūlkaḷ tamiḻar illamelām iṭam peṟuvataṟkum muṉ kuṟippiṭṭa 2000 - 2001 kālakkaṭṭattil veḷiyiṭappaṭṭa pāvāṇariṉ aṟivuk karuvūlaṅkaḷiyellām oṭṭu mottamāka cērttu orē vīccil pāvāṇar tamiḻk kaḷañciyam eṉum talippil  em aṟakkaṭṭaḷi veḷiyiṭukiṟatu.
maṟikkappeṟṟa māperum varalāṟṟiyum, citikkap peṟṟa oppuyarvuyaṟṟa moḻiyiyum koṇṭa tamiḻiṉattiṉ muṉṉēṟṟam karuti ivvaruntamiḻ putiyalkaḷi tamiḻ kūṟum nallulakiṟku  vaḻaṅka muṉ vantuḷḷōm. tamiḻ moḻiyi mūccākavum, pēccākavum, ceyalākavum koṇṭu orunāḷiṉ muḻuppoḻutum tamiḻākavē vāḻnta cempulaccemmal,  taṉittamiḻk katiravaṉ moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇar nūlkaḷi vāṅkip payaṉ koḷvīr.
iḷamiyilēyē potuttoṇṭilum, taṉittamiḻ iyakkat toṇṭilum nāṉ īṭupāṭu koḷvataṟkuk kāraṇamāka iruntavarkaḷ perumpulavar nakkīraṉ avarkaḷum, avartam iḷaval pulavar cittiravēlaṉ avarkaḷum āvar. ivarkaḷi ivveḷiyiṭṭiṉ vāyilāka naṉṟiyuṇarvōṭu niṉivu kūṟukiṟēṉ.  tanti periyāriṉ taṉmatippu iyakkak koḷki
kaḷālum, moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇariṉ taṉittamiḻ iyakkak koḷkikaḷālum īrkkappaṭṭavaṉ. attaku piṉpulattōṭu patippuppaṇiyil eṉ kālaṭic cuvaṭukaḷip patittu varukiṟēṉ.
kō. iḷavaḻakaṉ,
patippāḷar.


vāṉmaḻi vaḷaccurappu
āḻattuḷ āḻamāy akaḻntu taṅkam ēṉ eṭukkappaṭukiṟatu? āḻkaṭaluḷ ceṉṟu, uyirip paṇayam vittu muttuk kuḷikkap paṭuvatu ēṉ? pavaḻap pāḷaṅkaḷi eṭuppatu ēṉ? vayiram mutalām maṇikkulaṅkaḷi iṭiyaṟāmal tēṭit tēṭi eṭuppatu ēṉ?
aṇikalap poruḷkaḷ eṉpataṟku maṭṭumā? vīṭṭiṟku maṭṭumaṉṟi nāṭṭukkum īṭṭutaṟku ariya vippu vaḷamāka iruntu, nāṭṭiṉ poruḷiyal mēmpāṭṭukku aṭimaṇi
yākavum ivi iruppatāl tāṉē!
ivaṟṟiṉum mēlām vippu vaḷamum uṇṭō? uṇṭu! uṇṭu! avi cāṉṟōr nūlkaḷ!
kālam kālamāka aḷḷik koṇṭālum vaṟṟā vaḷamāyt tikaḻum aṟivuk karuvūlamām nūlkaḷ!
oṉṟik kaṟṟāl oru nūṟāy ōrāyiramāyp palkip perukat takka peṟaṟkariya tāy nūlkaḷām pēṟaminta nūlkaḷ! cēynūlkaḷi īṉavalla ceḻu nūlkaḷ!
nuṇ māṇ nuḻipulat tāy nūlkaḷāy iruppiṉum! avaṟṟit tāṅkiya tāḷkaḷ, ettakiya pēṇutal uṭiyavi eṉiṉum kāla veḷḷattil aḻiyak kūṭiyavi tāmē!
kalleḻuttē, katir veppālum kaṭaluppālum kāttup pēṇum kaṭappāṭillār kippaṭalālum aḻintu paṭṭami kaṇkūṭu tāṉē!
ēṭṭu varivukaḷ, nīrē neruppē nīṭitta kālamē puṟṟē pōṟṟā maṭamiyē eṉpavaṟṟāl aḻintu paṭṭamikku aḷavu tāṉum uṇṭō? makkaḷukku māṇap periya payaṉām nūlkaḷ, kaṟkum makkaḷ perukkattiṟkum, piṟantu perukivarum makkaḷ perukkattiṟkum takka vakiyil avarkaḷukkup pāṭṭaṉ pāṭṭiyar vitta paḻantēṭṭeṉṉa annūlkaḷ, mīḷamīḷat taṭṭillāmal kiṭikkac ceyya vēṇṭuvatu nūṟpatippar tavirāk kaṭamiyallavō!
akkaṭamiyi avarkaḷ kālantōṟum kaṭappāṭākak koṇṭu ceyya, āḷum aracum vāḻum aṟivarum arunnuṇiyātal tāṉē kaṭṭāyat tēvi! ivvāṟu patipparum aṟivarum āḷnarum tattam mūccuk kaṭaṉāka, nūṟkoṭi puritalukku mēṟkoṭi illiyām!
moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇar nūlkaḷ oṉṟā iraṇṭā? namakku eṭṭātavi pōka eṭṭiya nūlkaḷ 32. kaṭṭuri poḻivu mutalām vakiyāl kiṭṭiya tiraṭṭu nūlkaḷ 12. iṉṉum eṭṭāta tokuppum aṅkoṉṟum iṅkoṉṟum vara vāyppuṇṭu!
kaṭita varivu pā varivu eṉa mēlum perukavum vāyppuṇṭu!
ivaṟṟi ellām āyiram acciṭṭu avvaḷavil niṉṟu viṭṭāl, tēṭuvārkkut tēṭum poḻutil vāykkum tiruvākat tikaḻak kūṭumō?
ātalāl, cēy nūlkaḷukku mūlamām tāynūlkaḷ, kālam tōṟum vāṉmaḻi vaḷac curappāka veḷippaṭa vēṇṭum kaṭṭāyam uṇarntu kaṭami puriyum vīṟuṭiyār vēṇṭum! mika vēṇṭum!
ittakiya viḻumiya vīṟuṭiyar - iṉamāṉac cemmal - tamiḻp pōrāḷi - tiruttaku kō. iḷavaḻakaṉār, tām muntu muḻutuṟak koṇarnta pāvāṇar paṭippukaḷi mīḷamīḷat tamiḻulāk koḷḷa vikkum muṉ muyaṟciyāy, im mīḷpatippi vaḻaṅkukiṟār! tamiḻulakam payaṉ koḷvatāka! payaṉ ceyvatāka!
vāḻiya nalaṉē! vāḻiya nilaṉē!
iṉpa aṉpuṭaṉ
irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉ.



cāṉṟitaḻ
paṇṭita ñā. tēvanēyaṉār, pi.ō.el. potuvāka moḻinūl āyvu muṟikaḷip piṉpaṟṟit tamiḻccol ārāyccipaṟṟi eḻutiya nūlkaḷ tamiḻmoḻiyiṉ nīṇṭakālat tēviyiṉi niṟivu ceytaṉa. tammuṭiya iḷantik kālattilē talimik kaṇkāṇiyār tavattiru tirañcu (Trench) eḻutiya collārāycci, pērāciriyar mākkacu mullar eḻutiya moḻiyaṟiviyal, pērāciriyar cāycu eḻutiya oppiyal moḻinūl mutaliya āṅkiya nūlkaḷi yāmē peruviruppuṭaṉ paṭittukkoṇṭirukkumpōtu anta muṟiyil tamiḻc coṟkaḷi ārāya vēṇṭumeṉṟu virumpiṉēm. tamiḻp pēraṟiñarkaṭkup pulappaṭāmal maṟintu kiṭanta virivākavum viyappākavumuḷḷa tamiḻmoḻiyaṟivup parappu, paṇṭārakar kālṭuvel eḻutiya tirāviṭa moḻi
kaḷiṉ oppiyal āyvu nūlāl pulappaṭalāyiṟṟu. eṉiṉum paṇṭārakar kālṭuvel, aṟiyappaṭāta vaṭṭārattil ceyta
toru muyaṟciyātalāl tamiḻccoṟkaḷi yellām viṭāmal niṟivāka eṭuttārāyntuḷḷaṉar eṉṟu etirpārppataṟ
killi. ituvē moḻiyiyali ārāya vēṇṭu meṉṟu emmit tūṇṭiyatu. eṉavē `ñāṉacākaram (aṟivukkaṭal) eṉṉum emmuṭiya itaḻiṉ mutal tokutiyil at tuṟiyil oṉṟiraṇṭu kaṭṭurikaḷi eḻuti veḷiyiṭṭēm. āṉāl appōtu camayam, meypporuḷiyal, ilakkiya varalāṟu ākiya tuṟikaḷil emmuṭiya moḻiyārāyccit tuṟiyil toṭarntu īṭupaṭak kūṭavilli. āyiṉum takutiyuṭiya aṟiñar yārāvatu it tuṟiyil ārāyvataṟku muṉvarakkūṭumā eṉṟu etirpārttuk koṇṭiruntēm. appōtu yāḻppāṇam tiruttanti ñāṉappirakācartām ekipatiya moḻiyiyal ārāycci nūli emakku aṉuppivittār.  atu ōraḷavu emakku maṉaniṟivu aḷittatu. eṉiṉum moḻiyiyal ārāyccit
tuṟi mikavum virivum āḻamu muṭiyatātalāl paḻantamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷik kaṟṟu avaṟṟik koṇṭu naṉku purintukoḷḷum vakiyil mēlum moḻiyiyal ārāycci nūlkaḷ veḷivarutal vēṇṭumeṉṟu karutiṉēm. anta nērattil tiru. tēvanēyaṉār yām etirpārttati ēṟattāḻa muṟṟum niṟivēṟṟiyatu kaṇṭu perumakiḻvuṟṟēm. at tuṟiyil avar mikavum uḻippeṭuttu ārāyntu eḻutiyiruppavaṟṟit tamiḻ aṟiñarkaḷ naṉṟāka nampalām.
collārāyccit tuṟiyil tiru. tēvanēyaṉār oppaṟṟa taṉit tiṟamiyuṭiyavar eṉṟum, avarukku oppāka iruppavar arumiyākum eṉṟum yām uṇmiyākavē karutukiṉṟēm.
mēlum tiru. tēvanēyaṉār patiyac collum āciriyarum iṉpuṟuttum coṟpoḻivāḷarumāvar. pala tamiḻk kaḻaka āṇṭu viḻākkaḷil ematu talimiyiṉkīḻ avar coṟ
poḻivukaḷ āṟṟiyuḷḷār. avi kēṭṭārp piṇikkun takiyavāy amintu aviyōrik kiḷarcciyuṟac ceytu makiḻccik kaṭalil āḻttiṉa. mt® tUªâíiH¤J MuhŒ¢á brŒJtU« m¿P® Mjyhš mtiu¥ gÂÆš mk®¤J« vªj Ãiya¤J¡F« mtuhš ngU« òfG« »il¡f¥

tamiḻiyaṟ kaṭṭurikaḷ








1
centamiḻ varampīṭṭiṉ ciṟappu
ulakam muḻuvatum nōkkiṉ, moḻiyamippu, moḻi vaḷarppu, moḻikkāppu ākiya mūvaki moḻiviṉiyilum tali ciṟantavar kumarinilat tamiḻarē eṉpatu, teḷivākiṉṟatu.
vāyccōmpalālō, taṭpa veppanili paṟṟiya nila vēṟupāṭ ṭālō, iraṇṭiṉālumō, moḻi potumakkaḷ vāyil tirivatiyum, attiripiṟku elliyillāmiyiyum, kaṇṭa tamiḻaṟiñar, iyaṉilit tamiḻukkuc centamiḻ eṉṟum, tirinilit tamiḻukkuk koṭuntamiḻ eṉṟum peyariṭṭu, centamiḻiyē ulakuḷḷa aḷavum niliyāṉa aḷavi moḻiyākki viṭṭaṉar.
naṭimoḻi (dialect) yuṇmi paṇṭit tamiḻarukkut teriyā teṉṟum, vaṇṇaṉi moḻi nūl (Descriptive Linguistics) tōṉṟiya piṉpē naṭimoḻiyārāycci ulakeṅkum paraviya teṉṟum, mēli yarum avaric cārnta viyāpurikaḷum karutik koṇ ṭirukkiṉṟaṉar. tamiḻar ``tam moḻiyip piṟamoḻikaḷuṭaṉ maṭṭumaṉṟi, tam conta moḻik kuṭumpattic cērnta piṟamoḻikaḷuṭaṉkūṭa, oppunōkka orukālum muyaṉṟatilli. moḻik kuṭumpam eṉa oṉṟu uṇṭeṉṉum karutti, avarkaḷ eṉṟēṉum uṭkoṇṭatilli,'' eṉṟu kālṭu velārum eḻutiviṭṭār. āyiṉ, avar kālattil tolkāppiyap payiṟciyiṉmiyāl, avar kūṟṟu maṉṉikkat takkatē.
tamiḻ ulaka mutaṉmoḻiyā yiruttal pōṉṟu, moḻiyārāycciyum mutaṉmutal ammoḻiyilēyē tōṉṟiṟ ṟeṉpatu ki.mu. 7ām nūṟṟāṇṭileḻunta tolkāppiyam eṉṉum cārpiṟ cārpu nūlāl aṟiyak kiṭakkiṉṟatu. tolkāppiyam, vaṭacollum āriya ilakkiyamum āriyar marapum tavira, maṟṟellāvaṟṟiyum kumari nāṭṭu nilimi taḻuviyē kūṟuvatāl, akkālattil (aḵtāvatu āriyar varumuṉ) centamiḻ koṭuntamiḻ eṉṉum pākupāṭu tavira, iṟṟit tiraviṭa moḻikaḷ kūṭiyatu pōṉṟa oru moḻik kuṭumpam tōṉṟavilli eṉpati yaṟital vēṇṭum.
mēlum, āriyar vantapiṉ tamiḻ moḻi vaḷarcciyum moḻi yārāycciyum taṭukkappaṭṭu viṭṭataṉāl, kālṭuvelār kuṟikūṟal valiyaṟṟatākiṉṟatu.  
centamiḻ iyalpu
1.	tūymi allatu piṟamoḻic colliṉmi.
2.	tiruttam allatu eḻuttum collum olippilum eḻuttilum uriya vaṭiviliruttal.
3.	ilakkaṇam allatu coṟṟoṭaramippil vaḻuviṉmi.
immūviyalpum illatu koṭuntamiḻ.
teṟkē mūḻkippōṉa paḻam pāṇṭi nāṭṭiṉ teṉkōṭiyil irunta kumarimalit toṭariṉ teṉ koṭumuṭi, paṉimali yaḷavu tolivilirunta
taṉālum, iṟantupaṭṭa mutaliru kaḻaka nūlkaḷ āyirakkaṇakkiṉa vātalālum, akkālattut teṉcolvaḷam, kuṟinta pakkam iṉṟuḷḷatupōl  irumaṭaṅkiṉa
tāy iruntiruttal vēṇṭum. vaṇika vāyilāka vanta veḷināṭṭup poruḷkaṭkellām, uṭaṉuṭaṉ tamiḻp peyarkaḷ puṉiyappaṭṭaṉa.
centamiḻ nilam
centamiḻ vaḻaṅkiya nilam perumpālum pāṇṭi nāṭākavē yiruntatu. tamiḻ tōṉṟi vaḷarnta nilamum mukkaḻakamum irunta nāṭum pāṇṭi nāṭēyeṉṟum, avaṟṟip purantavar pāṇṭiyarē yeṉṟum aṟital vēṇṭum.
``viyi yāṟṟiṉ vaṭakkum marutayāṟṟiṉ teṟkum karuvūriṉ kiḻakkum maruvūriṉ mēṟkum'' eṉpatu, piṟkālattup poruḷil puṉin turiyām.
centamiḻc colvaki
centamiḻc coṟkaḷ iyaṟcol (Primitive), tiricol (Derivative) eṉa iruvakip paṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. e-ṭu: veḷ eṉpatu iyaṟcol; veḷḷam, veḷḷi, veḷḷi, veḷi, veḷil, veḷiṟu, veḷu eṉpaṉa tiricol.
koṭuntamiḻ nilamum ticiccollum
koṭuntamiḻ nilaṅkaḷil taṉicciṟappāka vaḻaṅkum coṟkaḷ (ikkālattu mēṉārikkam eṉṉum teluṅkac collum camāḷi eṉṉum kaṉṉaṭac collum pōlvaṉa), ēṟṟuk koḷḷappaṭṭup pala tici yiṉiṉṟum vantami paṟṟit ticiccol (Provincialism) eṉappaṭṭaṉa.
koṭuntamiḻ nilaṅkaḷ paṉṉiraṇṭākak kaṇakkiṭappaṭṭaṉa.
``centamiḻ cērnta paṉṉiru nilattum
taṅkuṟip piṉavē ticitcoṟ kiḷavi.''	(tol. ec. 4.)
tolkāppiyar kālattup paṉṉiru koṭuntamiḻ nilam evi yeṉpatu tiṭṭamākat teriyavilli.
``teṉpāṇṭi kuṭṭam kuṭamkaṟkā vēṇpūḻi 
paṉṟi yaruvā ataṉvaṭakku-naṉṟāya
cītam malāṭu puṉaṉāṭu centamiḻcēr
ētamil paṉṉirunāṭ ṭeṇ''
``ciṅkaḷam cōṉakam cāvakam cīṉam tuḷukkuṭakam
koṅkaṇam kaṉṉaṭam kollam teluṅkam kaliṅkam vaṅkam
kaṅkam makatam kaṭāram kavuṭam kaṭuṅkucalam
taṅkum pukaḻttamiḻ cūḻpati ṉēḻpuvi tāmiviyē.''
eṉṉum piṟkālattup pāṭalkaḷum, avaṟṟit taḻuviya
``centamiḻ nilañcēr paṉṉiru nilattiṉum
oṉpatiṟ ṟiraṇṭiṉil tamiḻoḻi nilattiṉum
taṅkuṟip piṉavē ticicco leṉpa''	(naṉ. 273)
eṉṉum nūṟpāvum, koḷḷattakkaṉa valla avaṟṟāl aṟiyak kiṭappatellām, orukālattil ilaṅkiyuṭpaṭa intiyā muḻutum tamiḻē vaḻaṅkiṟṟu eṉpatē. 
centamiḻ varampīṭṭiṉ naṉmi
1. moḻikeṭāmi
kumarināṭṭu moḻiyākiya tamiḻ oṉṟē, iṉṟu palvēṟu vakiyil tirintu patiṉeṇ moḻiyākap pirintuḷḷatu.
e-kā:
tamiḻ	maliyāḷam	teluṅku	kaṉṉaṭam	tuḷu
yāṉ, nāṉ	ñāṉ	nēṉu	nāṉu	yāṉu
oṉṟu	oṉṉu	oṇṭu, okaṭṭi	ontu	oñci		         vā	vā	rā	pā	pā
maruntu	maruṉṉu	mantu	martu	martu
iṅṅaṉam coṟkaḷellām tiriyātiruntiruppiṉ, tiraviṭa moḻikaḷellām iṉṟum tamiḻākavē vaḻaṅkum.
tamiḻiluḷḷa oruporuṭ pala coṟkaḷuḷ ovvoṉṟi ovvoru tiraviṭamoḻi terittāḷvatum, tamiḻiṉ tiraviṭat tāymiyik kāṭṭum.
e-kā :	maliyāḷam	teluṅku	kaṉṉaṭam
	vīṭu	illu(il)	maṉe(maṉi)
tiraviṭamoḻikaḷ pēccu vaḻakkil tirintiruppiṉum, ilakkiya vaḻakkilēṉum tamiḻip piṉpaṟṟiyiruntiruppiṉ iṉṟuḷḷa vēṟupāṭum māṟupāṭum nērntirā. iṟṟit tamiḻ nāṭṭiṟ pēccu vaḻakku iṭattiṟ kēṟpa vēvvēṟu vakiyil tirintiruppiṉum, ilakkiya vaḻakkiṟ cen tamiḻik kaṭippiṭippatāl, nāṭumuḻutum tamiḻnāṭeṉṟē vaḻaṅkutal kāṇka.
2. nilaṅkuṉṟāmi
centamiḻ koṭuntamiḻākat tirintu piṉṉarp palvēṟu moḻi kaḷākap pirintu pōṉataṉāl, orukālattiṟ paṉimalivari paraviyirunta tamiḻnilam paṭippaṭiyākac curuṅki, iṉṟu cēranāṭuñ cērātu vaṭacōḻa mākiya toṇṭināṭum muṇṭamākiyuḷḷatu. kaṭikkaḻakak kālattil vēṅkaṭak kōṭṭam vari orumoḻi nāṭāka irunta nilam, iṉṟu tamiḻ, teluṅku, kaṉṉaṭam, tuḷu, kuṭakam, tuṭavam, kōttam, paṭakam, maliyāḷam, koṅkaṇi eṉṉum orupāṉmoḻi nāṭākap pirintuḷḷatu.
3. iṉavoṟṟumi
moḻi yoṟṟumiyālēyē iṉavoṟṟumi nilittu niṟkum. moḻi vēṟupāṭṭāl iṉa vēṟupāṭum ataṉāl oṟṟumiyiṉmiyum, iṉṟu intiyetirppup pōrāṭṭattil viḷaṅkit tōṉṟukiṉṟatu. tiraviṭamoḻiyār camaṟkirutattip pōṟṟuvatum tamiḻip puṟakkaṇippa tum moḻi vēṟupāṭṭiṉ viḷivē.
4. vērpporuḷ maṟiyāmi
``ellāc collum poruḷkuṟittaṉavē,'' eṉṟu collakkūṭiya moḻi, ivvulakil tamiḻ oṉṟē. atu centamiḻ varampil niṟpatāl, atu tōṉṟi aimpatiṉāyiram āṇṭukaḷāyiṉum, āyirakkaṇakkāṉa aṭiccoṟkaḷum iṇippuc coṟkaḷum iṟantupaṭṭum, iṉṉum ataṉ perumpāṟ coṟkaḷ vērpporuḷ kāṭṭi niṟkiṉṟaṉa. piṟamoḻika ḷellām tirimoḻikaḷātalāl, avaṟṟiṉ coṟkaṭku vērpporuḷum mūlamum teriyātu. vaṭamoḻiyāḷar vaṭacoṟkaḷi iṭukuṟi, karaṇiyakkuṟi eṉa iruvakiyāyp pirittatōṭu, mēliyarum ellā moḻikaḷum iṭukuṟic coṟṟokutikaḷē eṉṟu muṭivuceytu, avvaṭippaṭiyil vaṇṇaṉi moḻinūliyum tōṟṟi vaḷarttu varukiṉṟaṉar.
col iyaṉiliyil mūlapporuḷ kāṭṭuvatiyum tiriniliyiṟ kāṭṭāmiyiyum, kīḻvarum eṭuttukkāṭṭiṟ kāṇka.
iyaṉili	tirinili
iṭikaḻi	ṭēkaḻi - rēḻi (kocci)
	ṭēkaḻi - tēkalī(va)
5. varalāṟṟutteḷivu
coṟkaḷ vērpporuḷ kāṭṭiṉāltāṉ moḻivaralāṟṟi aṟiyamuṭiyum. tamiḻ oṉṟē iyaṉmoḻiyātaliṉ, moḻivaralāṟṟut tiṟavukōl atilēyē āḻap putintu kiṭakkiṉṟatu.
6. aḻakuṭimi
``kalvikkaḻaku kacaṭaṟa moḻital'' ōtima (aṉṉa) naṭiyiṉum mātar naṭiyiṉum uyarnta aḻakuḷḷatu, maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ naṭiyākiya centamiḻ naṭi.
mēliyar, inti rōppiya moḻik kuṭumpattiṟku mūlamāṉa tamiḻi aṭippaṭiyākak koḷḷātu āriyattiyē koṇṭārāyntu, kōṭṭic cuvaril muṭṭiya kuruṭarpōl iṭarppaṭṭu, tāymoḻiyum kiḷimoḻiyum eṉṉum muṟimiyiṉṟi, taṉiyāḷ naṭimoḻi (Personal dialect), kuḻu naṭimoḻi (Group dialect), vakuppu naṭimoḻi (Communal or Class dialect), toḻil naṭimoḻi (Professional dialect), iṭa naṭimoḻi (Local dialect), vaṭṭāra naṭimoḻi (Regional dialect) eṉa ovvoru perumoḻiyiyum pala naṭimoḻikaḷākap pakuttu, ārāyntu nūlāka veḷiyiṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉar.
cilar ulaka vaḻakkiṟkum (Colloquial dialect) kocci vaḻakkiṟkum (Slange usage) vēṟupāṭu teriyātu, pēccu moḻiyiyum kavaṉittal vēṇṭumeṉṟu colli varukiṉṟaṉar. tamiḻil uyarntōr pēccu vaḻakkē ulaka vaḻakkām. 
``vaḻakkeṉap paṭuva tuyarntōr mēṟṟē
nikaḻcci avarkaṭ ṭāka lāṉa''	(tol. marapiyal. 93)
eṉṟu tolkāppiyaṅ kūṟutal kāṇka. ellā vakiyilum , mēṉmakkaḷ naṭiyiyē kīḻmakkaḷ piṉpaṟṟal vēṇṭum. kīḻmakkaḷ pēcci oppuk koḷḷavēṇṭumeṉiṉ, kīḻmakkaḷ oḻukkattiyum oppukkoḷḷa vēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉaṅ koḷḷiṉ, āṭciyum kāvalum vaḻakkut tīrppum kuṟṟat taṇṭaṉiyum vēṇṭiyatēyilli.
``karuttaṟivipput tāṉē moḻiyiṉ payaṉ! vªeilÆ‰ ngádh by‹d?'' eṉpar cilar. atu, ``paciyip pōkkuvatu tāṉē uṇaviṉ payaṉ! v§‡d« (gšJy¡fhJ« FËahJ« mLfy¤â‰FŸ ifÆ£L«) c©lhby‹d?'' eṉṟu viṉavuvatu pōṉṟirukkiṉṟatu. āṟaṟivu paṭitta nākarika māntaṉ evviṉi ceyyiṉum, tiruntiya muṟiyilēyē ceytal vēṇṭum.
moḻikkuc cemmi varampiṭātu vāypōṉa pōkkellām pēccut tiriyaviṭiṉ, atu caṇṭik kutiripōl ōriṭattu nillātu kāṭumēṭāy iḻuttuc cellum; iṟutiyiṟ kuḻikkuḷḷum taḷḷum. putupputu naṭi tōṉṟip paḻanaṭi vaḻakku vīḻum. paṇṭi ilakkiyam payaṉaṟṟuppōm. āyiram āṇṭiṟku muṟpaṭṭa āṅkila ilakkiyam, iṉṟu ārāycciyāḷarkkē payaṉpaṭukiṉṟatu. īrāyiram āṇṭukaṭku muṟpaṭṭa tamiḻilakkiyamō, iṟṟiyilakkiyam pōl ellārkkum payaṉpaṭukiṉṟatu. ātalāl, centamiḻ marapi ettuṇip pāṭupaṭṭum poruṭ celaviṭṭum pōṟṟik kāttal vēṇṭum.
``nilantī nīrvaḷi vicumpō ṭintum
kalanta mayakkam ulakam ātaliṉ
irutiṇi aimpāl iyalneṟi vaḻāamit
tirivil colloṭu taḻāal vēṇṭum''	(tol. marapiyal, 90)
``marapunili tiriyiṉ piṟitu piṟitākum.''	(kṣa 92)
āriyarāl mūvāyiram āṇṭum, ayalaracukaḷāl munnūṟāṇṭum, viyāpurikaḷāl aimpāṉāṇṭum citikkappaṭiṉum, iṉṟum tamiḻi aḻiyātu kāttatu ataṉ cemmi varampē. tamiḻ ellā vakiyilum oppuyarvaṟṟa taṉimoḻi. ataṉ uyirnāṭit taṉmi tūymi.
2
teṉmoḻi
ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil vāḻntirunta maṉōṉmaṇīyam āciriyar cuntarampiḷḷiyavarkaḷ,
``kaṉṉaṭamum kaḷiteluṅkum kaviṉmaliyā ḷamun tuḷuvum 
uṉṉutarat tutitteḻuntē oṉṟupala āyiṭiṉum
āriyampōl ulaka vaḻak kaḻintoḻintu citiyāvuṉ
cīriḷamit tiṟam viyantu ceyaṉmaṟantu vāḻttutumē''
eṉṟu tamiḻiṉ tiraviṭat tāymiyiyum, 
patiṉeṭṭām nūṟṟāṇṭil vāḻntilaṅkiya civañāṉamuṉivar, 
``irumoḻikkum kaṇṇutalār mutaṟkuravar iyalvāyppa
irumoḻiyum vaḻippaṭuttār muṉivēntar iciparappum
irumoḻiyum āṉṟavamē taḻīiyiṉār eṉṟāliv
ïUbkhÊí« Ãfbu‹W« ïj‰ifa« csnjnah?''
eṉṟu tamiḻiṉ camaṟkirutattiṟkotta taṉmiyiyum,
patiṉēḻām nūṟṟāṇṭil vatintirunta karuṇippirakācar,
``maṟimutaṟ kiḷanta vāyāṉ matimukiḻ muṭittavēṇi
iṟivartam peyarināṭṭi ilakkaṇam ceyyappeṟṟē
aṟikaṭal varippiṟpāṭi aṉittumveṉ ṟāriyattō
ṭuṟaḻtaru tamiḻtteyvatti uṇṇiṉin tēttalceyvām''
eṉṟu tamiḻ āriyattiṟ koppāyiruppatōṭu ataṉoṭu māṟupaṭṭu uyarviṟkup pōrāṭu niliyiyum,
patiṉāṟām nūṟṟāṇṭil tikaḻntirunta tiruviḷiyāṭaṟ purāṇa āciriyar parañcōti muṉivar, 
``kaṇṇu taṟperuṅ kaṭavuḷuṅ kaḻakamōṭamarntu
paṇṇu ṟatterin tāyntavip pacuntamiḻēṉi
maṇṇi ṭiccila ilakkaṇa varampilā moḻipōl
v©Â il¥gy ïlªjjh v©zî« gLnkh!''
eṉṟu tamiḻukku vaṭamoḻikkillāta ilakkaṇa varampuṇmiyiyum, oru paḻantamiḻppulavar, 
``ōṅka liṭivan(tu) uyarntōr toḻaviḷaṅki
ēṅkolinīr ñālat tiruḷakaṟṟum---āṅkavaṟṟuḷ
miṉṉēr taṉiyāḻi veṅkatiroṉ ṟēṉiyatu
taṉṉē rilāta tamiḻ''
eṉṟu tamiḻiṉ ulaka mutaṉmiyiyum ilakkiya muṉmiyiyum, palapaṭap pārāṭṭic ceṉṟaṉar.
iṅkilāntiṉiṉṟu teṉṉāṭu vantu vāḻnāḷmuḻutum tamiḻiyum tirāviṭa moḻikaḷiyum kaṟṟāynta kālṭuvel kaṇkāṇiyārum, tamiḻ vaṭamoḻit tuṇiyiṉṟit taṉittu vaḻaṅkavum, taḻittōṅkavum vallateṉṟum, tirāviṭa moḻikkuṭumpam ulaka mutaṉ moḻikku mika neruṅkiyateṉṟum makkaḷiṉ mutaṟ peṟṟōr moḻiyiṉiṉṟu vaḻivaḻi vantu vaḻaṅkum oru coṟṟokutiyit taṉṉakattuk koṇṭuḷḷa teṉṟum, teḷivākak kūṟiyuḷḷār.
iṅṅaṉamiruntum ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil tōṉṟiya oru puṉciṟu putukkalavi moḻiyiṉum tāḻvākak karutappaṭṭu varukiṉṟatu; ilakkaṇa vilakkiyac cemmi nirampi eṇṭiciyum paravi eṉṟu muḷa teṉṟamiḻk kivviḻitakavip pōkki, ataṉi mīṇṭum ariyaṇiyil amarttu taṟkē eḻuntatu ivvitaḻ.
kula mata kaṭci nilaccārpiṉṟit tūya moḻittoṇṭē iv itaḻ mēṟkoṇṭuḷḷamiyiṉ, akam, nāṭu, pulam, poḻil varippu mutaliya iṭapporuḷīṟiṉṟit tūya moḻip peyarē itaṟku iṭappeṟṟuḷḷatu.
tayir pāliṉiṉṟu tirintuḷḷatupōl tiraviṭa moḻikaḷ tamiḻiṉiṉṟu tirintu ōraḷavu āriyat taṉmi aṭintuviṭṭamiyālum, vaṭavēṅkaṭan teṉkumari yiṭippaṭṭa paṇṭit tamiḻc coṟkaḷē, teṉṉāṭṭut tiraviṭa moḻikaḷil ciṟappākap paḻañcēranāṭṭu moḻi yākiya maliyāḷattil aṭippaṭiyāka maṇṭikkiṭappatālum, iṟṟic cōḻa pāṇṭi nāṭṭu moḻiyiyum teṉṉāṭṭut tiraviṭattiṉ tamiḻp pakutiyiyum oruṅkē taḻuvutaṟkē `teṉmoḻi' yeṉṉum peyar ivvitaḻiṟku iṭappeṟṟateṉka,
ākavē `tamiḻ' eṉpatu tamiḻi maṭṭum kuṟikkumeṉṟum, `tiraviṭam' eṉpatu tamiḻiṉiṉṟu tirinta piṟamoḻikaḷiyē kuṟikku meṉṟum, `teṉmoḻi', eṉpatu avviraṇṭiyum kuṟikkumeṉṟum vēṟupāṭaṟintu koḷka.
3
tamiḻukku āṅkila naṭpum vaṭamoḻip pakiyum
eppōtum, aṇmiyiruppu aṉpuṟaviyum cēymiyiruppu ayaṉmi pakiyiyum kāṭṭā. oruvarkku aṭutta vīṭṭār piṟavip pakivarākavum ayal nāṭṭār uyir naṇparākavum irukkalām.
uḷḷū riruntuntam 	uḷḷattaṉ pillārēl
kaḷḷaviḻ cōliyāṅ kāṭṭuḷḷār kāṭṭuḷḷum
uḷḷattaṉ puḷḷārēl uṟṟārvāḻ nāṭṭuḷḷum
naḷḷi naṭuvū ruḷār.	(214)
eṉpatu aṟaneṟiccāram.
oru nāṭṭu allatu iṉattu makkaḷiṉ uḷḷatteḻuṅ karuttu kaḷit terivikkum coṟṟokutiyē moḻiyātaliṉ, iṅku makkaḷukkuc coṉṉatu moḻikaṭkum okkum.
āṅkilam ēṟattāḻa āṟāyiram kalliṟku appāluḷḷa ayal nāṭṭu moḻiyātalālum, vaṭamoḻi nam nāṭṭilēyē tōṉṟi vaḻaṅki varuvatālum, āṅkilam tamiḻukku ayaleṉṟum vaṭamoḻi tamiḻukku uṟaveṉṟum, oru tavaṟāṉa karuttu iruntu varukiṉṟatu.
āṅkilattiṉ uṇmi veḷiyīṭum vaṭamoḻiyiṉ uṇmi maṟippum:
āṅkilar 16ām nūṟṟāṇṭiliruntu kalattuṟiyiṟ ciṟantu kaṭalkaṭantu, ulakattiṉ pala pakutikaḷilum tam āṭciyi niṟuviṉatiṉāl, āṅkāṅkuḷḷa makkaḷiṉ moḻikaḷiṉiṉṟu vēṇṭumaḷavu coṟkaḷik kaṭaṉ koṇṭu tam moḻiyi vaḷappaṭuttiyuḷḷaṉar. ivvuṇmiyi avarē tam coṟ kaḷañciyaṅkaḷil (akarātikaḷil) teḷḷat teḷivākak kāṭṭiyuḷḷaṉar. 
e-kā :
aṇikkaṭṭu - Anicut
āṉikoṉṟāṉ - Anaconda
kaṭṭumaram - Catamaran
tēkku - Teak
paccili - Pachouli
miḷakut taṇṇīr - Mulliga tawny
veṟṟili - Betel
āṉikoṉṟāṉ eṉpatu ilaṅkiyil āṉiyik kollat takka oru perum pāmpiṟkup peyareṉṟum, piṉpu atu teṉamerikkā viluḷḷa oru perum pāmpiṟkum iṭappaṭṭateṉṟum, kīṟṟu (Skeat) eṉṉum āṅkilac coṟ piṟappiyalakarātiyāciriyar varintuḷḷār.
tēkku, veṟṟili eṉpavi maliyāḷac collākak kuṟikkap paṭṭuḷa. maliyāḷam eṉpatu paḻañ cēranāṭṭut tamiḻ eṉpati aṟika.
iṉi, Amuck (Amoq) eṉpatu maliyāc colleṉṟum, Taboo (Tapu) eṉpatu pāliṉīciyac colleṉṟum, uyarvillāmoḻic coṟkaḷikkūṭa uḷḷavāṟē veḷiyiṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉar. āyiṉ, vaṭamoḻiyāḷarō, vaṭamoḻiyil aintiliru pakuti tamiḻāyiruntum, oru collikkūṭat tamiḻeṉṟu oppukkoḷḷāmal vaṭamoḻi tēva moḻiyeṉṟum. atu piṟa moḻiyiṉiṉṟu kaṭaṉ koḷḷāteṉṟum, intiyap paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷi ēmāṟṟi vantirukkiṉṟaṉar, ataṟkēṟpa. avar vaṭamoḻiyiluḷḷa teṉcoṟkaḷiyellām tavaṟākap pirittu, poruntap poyttalākavum poruntāp poyttalākavum poruḷ kūṟiyirukkiṉṟaṉar. 
e-ṭu :	ulaku:
	ulam = uruṭci, tiraṭci, uruṇṭakal
	ulamvā - ulamā, ulamarutal = cuḻalutal
	ulakki = uruṇṭa peruntaṭi
	ulaṇṭu = uruṇṭunīṇṭa puḻu
	ulam = ulavu, ulavutal=cuṟṟutal, tirital
	ulavi = cuṟṟi vīcuṅ kāṟṟu.
	ulā =cuṟṟi varutal, aracaṉ nakari valamākac cuṟṟi varutal
	ulā - ulāvu, ulāvutal = cuṟṟittirital
	ulā - ulāttu, ulāttutal = cuṟṟit tirital
	ulā - ulāñcu, ulāñcutal = tali cuṟṟutal
	ulam-ulavu-ulaku=uruṇṭiyāṉatu, katiravaṉic cuṟṟi 								varuvatu
	ulaku-ulakam. o.nō: kaṭuku-kaṭukam
aṇṭam, kōḷam, Globe, Sphere mutaliya piṟa allatu piṟamoḻic coṟkaḷum ulakattik kuṟippatu uruṭcipaṟṟiyē.
ulakam eṉpatu vaṭamoḻiyil lōkka (loka) eṉṟu tiriyum, ataṟku lōk eṉpati vērākakkoṇṭu pārkkappaṭṭa iṭam eṉṟu poruṭkāraṇaṅ kāṭṭuvar. lōk eṉpataṟku Look eṉṉum āṅkilac col iṉamām. ulaku allatu ulakam eṉṉum coṟkut tamiḻ vērp poruḷā vaṭamoḻi vērp poruḷā, etu poruntum eṉṟu pakuttaṟivuṭiyār kaṇṭu koḷka.
lōkka eṉpatu lōk eṉṟu intiyiṟ kuṟukum.
aiyaṉ:
ai eṉpatu talivaṉik kuṟikkum peyar.
``eṉ ai muṉ nillaṉmiṉ' eṉpatu kuṟaḷ	(771)
talivaṉ eṉiṉum periyōṉ eṉiṉum okkum. aracaṉ, āciriyaṉ, tanti, tāy, aṇṇaṉ eṉa oruvarkku aintu periyōr uḷar. avarkkellām ai eṉpatu potuppeyar. tāyik kuṟikkumpōtu atu āy eṉṟu tiriyum. ai eṉpatu `aṉ' īṟupeṟiṉ aiyaṉ eṉṟākum. aiyaṉ eṉpataṟku aiyi eṉpatu peṇpāl, aṇṇaṉik kuṟikkum pōtu ai, aiyaṉ eṉṉum coṟkaḷ taṉṉi, tamiyaṉ eṉat taṉ, tam eṉṉum muṉṉoṭṭuppeṟum.
tamiḻaruṭ palavakuppār, avaruḷḷum ciṟappākat tāḻttappaṭṭavar, tantiyi aiyaṉ allatu aiyā (viḷivaṭivam) eṉṟē aḻikkiṉṟaṉar, āciriyar evvakuppārāyiruppiṉum, avari aiyar eṉṟaḻippatu vaṭārkkāṭṭu āmpūr vaṭṭattār vaḻakku.
periyōri yellām aiyā eṉṟu kuṟippatum viḷippatum toṉṟutoṭṭut tamiḻar vaḻakkam. makkaḷuṭ periyār muṉivar eṉṉum karuttuppaṟṟi, avaric ciṟappāka aiyar eṉpatu tamiḻ nūṉ marapu.
``aiyar yāttaṉar karaṇam eṉpa.''	(tol. 1091)
eṉpatu tolkāppiyam
piṅkala nikaṇṭil muṉivarippaṟṟiya 3ām pakuti aiyar vaki eṉappeyar peṟṟuḷatu.
muṉivarilum periyavar teyvaṅkaḷātaliṉ, teyvaṅkaṭkum kaṭavuṭkum aiyaṉ eṉṉum peyar vaḻaṅkum.
aiyaṉ = cāttaṉ (aiyaṉār), kaṭavuḷ.
aiyi = kāḷi, malimakaḷ.
iṅṅaṉa mellām toṉṟutoṭṭu vaḻaṅkum aiyaṉ eṉṉum teṉcolli, ārya eṉṉum vaṭacoṟciti veṉpar vaṭavar.
vaṭavi:
vaṭavi allatu vaṭanti eṉpatu vaṭam (vaṭakku) eṉṉum ticip peyariṉiṉṟu tirintu, vaṭamuṉiyattil (turuvattil) avvappōtu tōṉṟum neruppi allatu oḷiyik kuṟikkum tūya teṉcol. tāyumāṉavar iti vaṭavaṉal eṉṟē kuṟippiṭukiṉṟār. itaṟku uttaram (vaṭakku) allatu uttaramaṭaṅkal eṉṟum peyar. itaṟku nērāṉa Aurora borealis eṉṉum ilattīṉ collum, vaṭakkattu neruppu allatu oḷi eṉṉum poruḷatē. vaṭavi eṉpati vaṭavā eṉattirittu mukam eṉṉum collic cērttu, peṭṭikkutiri mukattil tōṉṟuvatu eṉṟu vaṭanūlār poruṭkāraṇaṅ kūṟuvatu, ettuṇi iḻitakavāṉa ceyal eṉṟu kaṇṭukoḷka.
iñci:
iñcutal = uḷḷiḻuttal. nīr iñciyiruppatu iñci. iñci, vērvakiyātaliṉ ati iñcivēr eṉpatu marapu. kaṭikkaḻakak kālattil tamiḻakattiṉiṉṟu kirēkka nāṭṭiṟku ēṟṟumatiyāṉa iñci vēr kirēkkattil Ziggi beris eṉṟum, ilattīṉil Zingiber, gingiber eṉṟum, aḻikkappeṟṟatu. piṉṉar āṅkilattil ginger eṉṟāyiṟṟu.
vēta allatu intiya āriyarkkum veḷināṭṭu ēṟṟumatikkum yātoru toṭarpumilli. āyiṉum, Ziggi beris eṉṉum kirēkka vaṭivatti cruṅkavēr eṉat tirittu, vilaṅkiṉ kompupōṉṟatu eṉat tuṇintu kūṟuvar vaṭamoḻivāṇar. cruṅkam=kompu. vēra=uṭampu. 
cāyuṅkālam:
poḻutu cāykiṉṟa kālam cāyuṅkālam. ic col cāyaṅkālam, cāyṅkālam eṉa maruvum. cāyaṅkālam eṉpatiṉ varumoḻiyi nīkkic cāyam (sāyam) eṉa vittukkoṇṭu, muṭivatu eṉṟu poruṭ kāraṇaṅ kūṟuvar vaṭamoḻiyar. 
āmivaṭi:
āmiyōṭupōl mēluṅ kīḻum vaḷivuḷḷa vaṭi āmivaṭi. āmittāli, āmippalaki, āmippūṭṭu eṉpaṉavum ikkaraṇiyam (kāraṇam) paṟṟiyaviyē. āmi eṉpati āma eṉattirittu, naṉṟāy vēkātatu eṉak karaṇiyaṅkāṭṭiṉ, pittarum pētiyarumtāṉ oppuk koḷḷamuṭiyum. 
uvaṇam:
u eṉpatē uyarattik kuṟikkum ōr ōreḻuttu vērc col. uka, ucci, uttaram, umpar, uyar, ūrti, uṉṉu mutaliya coṟkaḷi nōkkuka. 
``ukampē uyartal''	(tol. 789)
eṉpatu tolkāppiyam.
uvaṇ = mēliṭam. uvaṇam = uyarcci. uvaṇi = tēvarulakam.
uvaṇam = paruntu, kaluḻaṉ (karuṭaṉ)
uyarappaṟakkum paṟavi uvaṇam eṉappaṭṭatu.
``cuîau¥ gwªjhY« C®¡FUÉ gUªjhFkh?'' eṉpatu paḻamoḻi.
uvaṇam - cuvaṇam = kaluḻaṉ
o-nō: uḻal - cuḻal, uruḷ - curuḷ
cuvaṇattic cuparṇa eṉṟu tirittu, cu+parṇa eṉṟu pirittu, nalla ilipōṉṟa ciṟakuṭiyatu eṉṟu karaṇiyaṅkāṭṭuvatu, uttikkup poruntumā eṉṟu ūṉṟi nōkkuka.
muttu:
muttu-muttam. muttu ciṟiyatu; muttam periyatu. `am' oru perumip poruḷ īṟu (Augmentative Suffix) ō.nō: nili-niliyam, viḷakku-viḷakkam, mati-matiyam (muḻunilā) muṭṭuppōl uruṇṭiruppatu muttu. muṭṭu = muṭṭi. muttu-muṭṭu.
uruṇṭiyāyuḷḷa vitikaḷum mutteṉappaṭum.
e-kā: āmaṇakku muttu, kurukku muttu, vēppamuttu.
intiyāvil toṉṟu toṭṭu muttiṟkuc ciṟantatu pāṇṭināṭu.
``vēḻa muṭittu malināṭu mētakka
cōḻavaḷanāṭu cōṟuṭittu - pūḻiyarkōṉ
teṉṉāṭu muttuṭittu teṇṇīr vayaṟṟoṇṭi
naṉṉāṭu cāṉṟō ruṭittu''
eṉṟār auviyār. iṭikkaḻakappāṇṭiyar talinakarākiya kavāṭapurattiṉiṉṟu ceṉṟa muttu, cāṇakkiyar poruḷ nūliṟ pāṇṭiya kapāṭam eṉap peyar peṟṟatu.
iṅṅaṉamirukkavum, muttam eṉṉum colli mukta eṉṟu tirittu, cippiyiṉiṉṟu viṭutali peṟṟatu eṉṟu poruṭ karaṇiyaṅ kūṟiṉ, aṟivuṭiya tamiḻaṉ evaṉ nampuvāṉ?
iṉi, muḻuppūcaṉikkāyic cōṟṟil muḻukkuvatupōl, tamiḻ allatu tamiḻam eṉṉum colliyē, tramiḷa-tramiṭa-traviṭa eṉat tirittu āriyac collākka muyalpavar vēṟu eṉṉatāṉ ceyyār?
āṅkilam tamiḻt tūymi pēṇalum vaṭamoḻi tamiḻ vaḻakku vīḻttalum:
intiyā muḻuvatiyum ōrāṭcippaṭuttaṟkum, intiyarkku aṟiviyaṟ kalvi pukaṭṭaṟkum āṅkilar āṅkilatti āṭcimoḻi yākkiṉarē yaṉṟi, intiya moḻikaḷip puṟakkaṇittaṟkaṉṟu. vēta āriyarō, tammi nilattēvar (pūcurar) eṉṟum tam muṉṉōr moḻiyit tēva moḻi eṉṟum ēmāṟṟi, intiya nākarikamum civa māl mataṅkaḷum tamiḻaruṭi
yaviyāyum, tamiḻ tiraviṭat tāyum āriya mūlamumāyum, iruntum, tamiḻ vaḻipāṭṭiṟkutavāmoḻi eṉattaḷḷi viṭṭataṉāl, kaṭikkaḻakam kalintum paṇṭit tamiḻ nūlkaḷellām aḻintum, ēṟattāḻa mūvāyiram āṇṭukaḷākat tamiḻ vaḷarcciyaṟṟum, āyirakkaṇakkāṉa teṉcoṟkaḷ maṟintum vaḻakku vīḻntum, pōṉatoṭu, tamiḻarum tāḻttappaṭṭum, paltuṟiyil piḻip piḻantum, uyarnilikkalvi
yiṉṟiyum, taṉmāṉamum pakuttaṟivum neñcuramum kuṉṟiyum, pōyiṉar.

āṅkila vuṟavu nerukkamum vaṭamoḻi yuṟavut tolivum:
teṟkil muḻukippōṉa kumarikkaṇṭat tamiḻar vaṭakkē ceṉṟu tiraviṭarāyt tirintaṉar. avaruḷ oru cārār vaṭamēṟkāy airōppā ceṉṟu āriyarāy māṟiṉar. avvāriyaruḷ oru vakuppārē nāvalan tēyattiṟkut tirumpi vanta vēta āriyar. ivvaralāṟṟic cila coṟkaḷum avaṟṟiṉ tiripum kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa.

tiyūttāṉiyam eṉpatu, āṅkilam, cerumāṉiyam, taccam (Dutch), tēṉiyam (Danish), aiculāntiyam (Icelandic), mutaliya mēliyirōppiya moḻikaḷikkoṇṭa oru kiḷiyāriyak kuṭumpamākum.
uṇmi viṭutali:
āṅkilar āṭci nīṅkiṉatiṉāl maṭṭum tamiḻaṉ viṭutali yaṭintu viṭavilli. kīḻiyāriyamākiya vaṭamoḻikku aṭimippaṭṭuk kiṭakkum tamiḻ, viṭutaliyaṭintu, muṉpōl mīṇṭum kōyil vaḻipāṭṭu moḻiyāṉāloḻiya, tamiḻaṉukku viṭutaliyilli. uṇmiyil tēvamoḻiyeṉṟu ulakil orumoḻiyumilli. aṅṅaṉam oṉṟu irukkumāyiṉ, atu, ki. mu. aimpatiṉāyiram āṇṭukaṭkumuṉ kumarikkaṇṭattil tāṉē tōṉṟiyatāyum, vaṭamoḻikku mūlamāyumuḷḷa tamiḻāyttāṉirukka muṭiyum.
tamiḻ nāṭṭil virutunakar cilavakiyil ciṟanta nakarākum. virutu eṉpatu, paṭṭam, koṭi mutaliya veṟṟic ciṉṉaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟik kuṟikkum peyar. nāṭār kōṭṭikaḷuḷ nāyakamāṉatu virutunakar. nāṭār eṉpatu, nāṭāṉ eṉṉum colliṉ uyarvup paṉmiyāy, nāṭṭāṇmik kāraṉi allatu nāṭāḷiyik kuṟikkum peyar. nāṭaṉ-nāṭāṉ - nāṭār.
pērāyak (Congress) kaṭciyic cērnta tiruvāḷar kāmarācu nāṭārum, nērmik (Justice) kaṭciyic cērnta tiruvāḷar (V. V.) irāmacāmi nāṭārum, virutunakar vāṇar. ivaruḷ, muṉṉavar āṅkila aṭimittaṉatti akaṟṟiyavar; piṉṉavar tamiḻaṉiṉ uṇmi nilimiyaṟintu āriya aṭimittaṉattiyum akaṟṟa virumpukiṉṟavar. ātalāl, avar tamiḻar aṉivarālum pārāṭṭap peṟattakkavarāvar. innilavulakil avar nīṭuvāḻiyāy niṉṟu nilava, iṟivaṉ tiruvaruḷ poḻika.
4
tamiḻ taṉittiyaṅkumā?
tamiḻ varalāṟu tavaṟāy eḻutappaṭṭiruppatālum ataṉ ciṟappiyalpu talimit tamiḻp pērāciriyaṉmārālum aṟiyappaṭātiruppatālum, tamiḻ tavira ēṉi moḻikaḷellām piṟamoḻik kalappuṟṟiruppataṉālum, pala teṉ coṟkaḷ vaṭa coṟkaḷeṉak karutappaṭuvatālum, tamiḻ taṉittiyaṅkumā eṉṉum kēḷvi aṟiyār vāyilum aṟintavarkaḷ vāyilum nīṇṭa nāḷāyiruntu varukiṉṟatu.
ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil, kaḻaka (caṅka) nūṟkalviyum taṉit tamiḻuṇarcci yum maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ taṉit tamiḻt toṇṭum illāta kālattil, āṅkila nāṭṭiṉiṉṟu iṅku vantuteṉpāṇṭi nāṭṭiṟ kuṭipukuntu ari nūṟṟāṇṭākat tamiḻum tiraviṭamumāṉa teṉ moḻikaḷiyāyntu teṉmoḻi yoppiyavilakkaṇaṅ kaṇṭa maṟittiruk kālṭuvel kaṇkāṇiyār, tamiḻiṉ teṉmiyiyum muṉmiyiyum tāymiyiyum tūymiyiyum uḷḷavāṟuṇarāviṭiṉum, ``tamiḻ vaṭamoḻit tuṇi yiṉṟit taṉittu vaḻaṅkutal maṭṭumaṉṟit taḻittōṅkutaluṅ kūṭum'' eṉṟu tēṟṟampaṭak kūṟiyuḷḷār. aṅṅaṉaṅkūṟi mukkāl nūṟṟāṇṭiṟku mēlākiyum avar kālattiṉpiṉ moḻiyārāycci ettuṇiyō muṉṉēṟiyiruntum, iṉṉum avar kūṟṟil aiyuṟuvatum āyntu uṇmi
kāṇa maṟuppatum, tamiḻi vēṇṭāt taṉmiyiyē viḷakkukiṉṟatu.
kumarik kaṇṭa muḻukkiṉālum paṇṭit tamiḻilakkiya aḻiviṉā lum ettuṇiyō ulaka vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷum ceyyuḷ vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷum iṟantupaṭṭiruntum, iṉṉum tamiḻit taṉi moḻiyāy vaḷarkkakkūṭiya pallāyirac coṟkaḷum paṟpala coṟkaruvikaḷum tamiḻil iruntē varukiṉṟaṉa. vaḻakkaṟṟa tamiḻc coṟkaḷi vaḻakkāṟṟup paṭuttuvatum ik kālattiṟku vēṇṭiya putuc coṟkaḷi puṉintu koḷvatumākiya iru vaḻikaḷik kiyāḷiṉ, tamiḻ taṉittiyaṅka eḷḷaḷavun taṭiyilli.
1. vaḻakkaṟṟa teṉ coṟkaḷ
vaṭamoḻi tēvamoḻi yeṉṉum ēmāṟṟiyum paṇṭit tamiḻ vēntariṉ pētimiyiyum tuṇikkoṇṭu, āyirak kaṇakkāṉa vaṭa coṟkaḷ tamiḻil vēṇṭātu pukuttappaṭṭataṉ viḷivāka avaṟṟiṟku nērāṉa viḻumiya teṉ coṟkaḷ ciṟitum peritum muṟṟum vaḻakku vīḻttap
paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
e-kā :
vaṭacol	teṉcol
aṉṉam	ekiṉ, ōtimam
āṉmā	ātaṉ, uṟavi, pulampaṉ
āṉantam, kutūkalam, cantōcam	uvaki, kaḷippu, makiḻcci
cakuṉam	puḷ
cattiyam, nicam, vātavam	uṇmi, vāymi, mey(mmi)
cīraṇam	cerimāṉam
cuttam	tuppuravu
tantiram	valakkāram
tirupti	pontiki
pitirārccitam	mutu com
mēkam	mukil, kār, koṇṭal
lāpam	ūtiyam
naṭṭam	iḻappu
mittuṉaṉ	aḷiyaṉ
varucam	āṇṭu
tirī, puruṣar	āṭavar, peṇṭir
iṅkuk kuṟikkappaṭṭa vaṭa coṟkaḷ viḷaṅkutaṟ poruṭṭup perumpālum taṟpava vaṭiviṟ kāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
nakic cuviyi hāya racam eṉpatum, aviyic cata eṉpatum, paṇiviṭiyic cicuruṣi eṉpatum, tirumaṇattip pariṇayam eṉpatum, kuṭamūkku (kuṭanti), kuraṅkāṭutuṟi, ciṟṟampalam (tilli), paḻamali (mutukuṉṟam), mayilāṭutuṟi, maṟikkāṭu mutaliya centamiḻ nāṭṭūrttaṉit tamiḻp peyarkaḷi, muṟiyē, kumpakōṇam, kapitalam, citamparam, viruttācalam, māyūram, vētāraṇiyam eṉa vaṭa coṟkaḷāy ēṟkeṉavē, māṟṟiyiruppatum, ittakiya iḻi ceyali iṉṟum kiyāḷvatum, teṉṉāṭṭu vaṭamoḻiyāḷariṉ variyiṟanta vaṭamoḻi veṟiyiyaṉṟi vēṟetik kāṭṭum?
iṉṟu tamiḻil vaḻaṅkum vaṭa coṟkaḷ tamiḻiṟku vēṇṭiyavumalla; tamiḻar virumpiyavumalla. uṭampiṟkuṭ pukuntu ataṉukku ūṟuceyyum naccup puḻukkaḷiyum iṉṉāp poruḷkaḷiyum ataṉ nalattiṉ poruṭṭu nīkkuvatu pōṉṟē, vēṇṭā vaṭacoṟkaḷiyum tamiḻiṉiṉṟu vilakkutal vēṇṭum. itaṉit taṭuppavar puṟap pakivarum uṭ pakivarumāyēyiruttal vēṇṭum.
vaṭacoṟkaṭku iṅkuk kūṟiyatu piṟa coṟkaṭkum okkum.
2. putuc coṟpuṉivu
pala poruḷkaḷ pal vakiyil vēṟupaṭṭiruppiṉum, avaṟṟiṟ kellām potuvāka oru taṉmi kāṇakkiṭakkiṉṟatu. uyirikaḷ oṉṟu iṉṉoṉṟi allatu palavaṟṟip piṟappippatu pōṉṟē karuttukaḷum oṉṟu iṉṉoṉṟi allatu palavaṟṟit tōṟṟuvikkiṉṟaṉa. iv vīruṇmikaḷiyum kaṇṭa kumarik kaṇṭat tamiḻar. ōraṭiyiṉiṉṟē īṟṟu vēṟupāṭṭāṟ palvēṟu coṟkaḷit tirittu, avaṟṟāṟ paṟpala poruḷkaḷiyuṅ kuṟippittirukkiṉṟaṉar.
e - kā :

iṅkuk kāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷa coṟkaḷellām, veṇṇiṉattik kuṟikkum. veṇ eṉṉum orēyaṭiyiṉiṉṟu piṟantu veṇmik karutti aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭa palvēṟu kāṭcip poruḷkaḷiyuṅ karuttup poruḷkaḷiyuṅ kuṟippaṉavākum. ivvaṭiyiṉiṉṟu piṟanta vēṟu cila coṟkaḷumuḷa. avi virivañci iṅku viṭappaṭṭuḷa.
cila poruḷkaṭkup piṟavaṟṟiṟkillāta ciṟappiyalpuṇṭu. ataṉi yum nam muṉṉōrkaṇṭu, ataṟkēṟpap peyarkaḷi amittiruk kiṉṟaṉar.
e - kā : vāḻi (aṭi vaḻa vaḻaveṉṟiruppatu).
iṅṅaṉamē piṟa moḻiyāḷarum tattam moḻiyiṟ coṟkaḷi ākkiyirukkiṉṟaṉar. ākavē, āṅkilak kuṟiyīṭukaḷiṉ vērp poruḷiyō avaṟṟāṟ kuṟikkappaṭum poruḷkaḷiṉ ciṟappiyalpi yō aṟintu koḷḷiṉ, aṟiviyalum kammiyamum paṟṟiya ellāc coṟkaḷiyum eḷitāyt tamiḻil amittuk koḷḷalām coṟṟiri piṟkēṟṟa īṟukaḷ tamiḻil ērāḷamākavuḷḷaṉa. toḻiṟ peyarīṟukaḷ maṭṭum tal, al, am, ai, ki, vi, ku, pu, u, ti, ci, vi, uḷ, pāṭu, kāṭu, aravu, āṉi, mi, tu, il, āl, aṉi, aṉam, iṉam, aṇam, iṇam, aṭam, iṭam, aram, itam mutaliyaṉavāka muppatiṟkum mēluḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟoṭu, viṉi mutalīṟukaḷ ceyappaṭu poruḷīṟukaḷ mutaliya īṟṟuvakikaḷiyum, kuṟumip poruḷ, perumip poruḷ mutaliyaṉapaṟṟiya muṉ piṉ oṭṭu vakikaḷiyum, cērppiṉ ettuṇiyō palavākum. ilattīṉ kirēkkam mutaliya pala moḻikaḷiṉiṉṟu āṅkilam kaṭaṉ koṇṭuḷḷa īṟukaḷi yum oṭṭukkaḷiyum otta colluṟuppu vaḷam, tamiḻ oṉṟilēyē iyalpāka amintu kiṭakkiṉṟatu.
Electricity eṉṉum āṅkilac col, ampar eṉṉum naṟumaṇap poruḷik kuṟikkum electron eṉṟa kirēkkac colliṉiṉṟum piṟantatu. ampar eṉpatu tamiḻil miṉcāram eṉavum peṟum. 12ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉa tāṉa aṭiyārkku nallāruriyil ampar eṉṉuñ col vantiruttalāl, atiyum teṉ colleṉak koḷḷa iṭamuṇṭu. miṉṉāṟṟalik kuṟittaṟku ampar eṉṉuñ colliṉiṉṟu āmpariyam eṉṟoru collit tirittuḷḷaṉar putuccēri vāṇar. ceṉṉi nāṭṭut tamiḻil miṉcāram eṉṉuñ col ēṟkeṉavē puṉiyap peṟṟuḷḷatu. ati miṉ(ṉam) eṉṟum māṟṟik koḷḷalām.
Radio eṉṉum āṅkilac col, Radius eṉṉum ilattīṉ colliṉiṉṟu tirintatu. ilattīṉ poruḷ, taṇṭu, āri, katir eṉpaṉa. katir eṉṉum colliṉiṉṟu katiram allatu katiriyam eṉṟoru colli nām tirittuk koḷḷalām.
cila poruḷkaḷiṉ allatu kali muṟikaḷiṉ peyar, avaṟṟik kaṇṭu piṭitta āṭpeyarāl allatu iṭappeyarāl amintuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟi appaṭiyē varipeyarttu vittuk koḷḷalām.
e - kā :
Galvanism eṉṉum miṉṉākkamuṟi kālvaṉi (Galvani) eṉpavarāṟ kaṇṭu piṭikkappaṭṭatu.
Galvanization = kālvaṉittal. Galvanism kālvaṉiyam. Bauxite eṉṉum maṇ vaki pirāṉcu nāṭṭil  pākkacu (Baux) eṉṉum iṭattiṟ kaṇṭu piṭikkappaṭṭatu. atip pākkaci eṉṟu vari peyarttuk koḷḷalām.
Chemistry eṉṉum āṅkilac col, Alckemy eṉpataṉiṉṟu tirintatu. itu Al - kimia eṉṉum arapic colliṉ tiripu. Al = the (anta eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum muṉṉoṭṭu); Kimia eṉpatu Khemia eṉpataṉ tiripu. itu ekiptu (Egypt) nāṭṭup peyariṉ kirēkka vaṭivam. ākavē, Chemistry eṉpatik kemiyam eṉṟu collalām.
Chemist = kemiyaṉ, kemiyar
Chemical = kemiya.
cila poruṭ peyarkaḷ aḷavupaṟṟi amintuḷḷaṉa. Duoderum eṉpatu paṉṉiraṇṭu eṉṉum ilattīṉ eṇṇup peyar. atu āṅkilattil paṉṉiru viral (aṅkulam) aḷavuḷḷa ciṟu kuṭaṟ pakutiyik kuṟikkiṉṟatu. itip paṉṉīram eṉṟu moḻi peyarkkalām.
Furlong eṉṉum āṅkilac col paṭiccāl nīḷam eṉṉum poruḷatu. fur = furrow (paṭiccāl). paṭiccāl eṉpatu aṇippu eṉṟum collappaṭum. ākavē, furlong eṉpatip paṭiccāl eṉṟō, aṇippu eṉṟō, cāṉīḷam eṉṟō moḻi peyarkkalām.
cila peyarkaḷ uvamiyāku peyarāyuḷḷaṉa. Bacterium eṉṉum āṅkilac col ciṟu kuccuppōl tōṉṟum oru vaki nuṇ puḻuviṉ peyar. itu kuccu eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum baktron eṉṉum kirēkkac colliṉ kuṟumip poruḷ vaṭivāṉa bakterion eṉpataṉ tiripu. il eṉpatu tamiḻil oru kuṟumip poruṭ piṉṉoṭṭu (diminutive suffix).
e -ṭu :	toṭṭi - toṭṭil = ciṟu toṭṭi
	puṭṭi - puṭṭil = ciṟu puṭṭi
kuccu eṉpataṉoṭu ippiṉṉoṭṭic cērppiṉ, bacterium eṉpataṟku nērāṉa kuccil eṉṉum tamiḻccol ām. ciṟu vīṭṭik kuṟikkum kuccil eṉpatu kuṟṟil eṉpataṉ koccit tiripu. cilapeyarkaḷ paṉmaṭiyāku peyarkaḷāyuḷḷaṉa.
Mail eṉṉum āṅkilaccol pi eṉṉum aṭippaṭip poruḷatu. atu piṉpu, muṟiyē, pikkuḷ iṭum kaṭitaṅkaḷiyum avaṟṟik koṇṭu cellum puki vaṇṭiyiyum kuṟittatu. ākavē, Mail eṉpati añcal allatu añcali eṉṟu kuṟikkalām.
ellāc coṟkaḷiyum vērpporuḷ paṟṟiyē moḻi peyarkka vēṇṭiyatilli. poruḷkaḷiṉ ciṟappiyalpu nōkki ataṟkēṟpa oru peyariṭalām. immuṟiyilēyē, trin eṉpatip pukiviṭum ciṟappiyalpu paṟṟip puki vaṇṭi eṉak kuṟittaṉar tamiḻp potumakkaḷ. Trin eṉṉum āṅkilac col iḻu (Draw) eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum trab eṉṉum ilattīṉ mutaṉiliyiṉiṉṟu tirintatu. Cycle miti vaṇṭi eṉpar nelli nāṭṭār. ati īruruḷi eṉṉat tēviyilli.
paṇṭi nāṭkaḷil ayal nāṭukaḷiṉiṉṟu vanta ellāp poruḷ kaṭkum, avaṟṟiṉ ciṟappiyalpu paṟṟiyat tūya teṉ coṟ peyarkaḷi iṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉar. paṇpaṭṭa paḻan tamiḻp potumakkaḷ. karumpu cālit tīviṉiṉṟum, pukiyiliyum uruḷik kiḻaṅkum amerikka nāṭṭiṉiṉṟum, vāṉkōḻi turukki nāṭṭiṉiṉṟum vantavi. amerikkat taṉṉāṭṭuc coṟkaḷiṉ tiripāṉa tabaco (E. tobacco), Patata ( E. potato) eṉṉum icuppāṉiyac (Spanish) coṟkaḷit tamiḻ ēṟṟuk koḷḷa villi. immoḻiyuṇarcci piṟkālattil āriyattāṟkeṭṭatu.
cila coṟkaḷ āṅkilattilum tamiḻilum aṭipporuḷum vaḻip poruḷum ottuḷḷaṉa. Pen eṉṉum āṅkilac col, tūvu (feather) eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum Penna eṉṉum ilattīṉ coṟṟiripu. tūval eṉṉum tamiḻc collum attakiyatē.
āṅkilac coṟkaḷi moḻi peyarkkumpōtu vaṇṇaṉi (descriptive) muṟiyik kiyāḷakkūṭātu. Collector eṉpatit taṇṭalāḷar eṉṟē moḻi peyarttal vēṇṭum. māvaṭṭa āṭcit talivar eṉṟu kuṟippiṉ, Collector eṉpatiyum District Administrative Head allatu District Chief Administrator eṉṟu māṟṟal vēṇṭum. atu poruntāmi kāṇka. kāṭciyum karuttumākiya iruvakip poruḷ kaṭkum, coṟkaḷ oru maruṅku kāraṇakkuṟiyāy iruntāṟ pōtum.
ittakiya muṟikaḷik kiyāḷiṉ, ellā āṅkilak kuṟiyīṭu kaḷiyum tamiḻil moḻi peyarttu viṭalām. āṅkilak kuṟiyīṭuka ḷellām mika eḷiya muṟiyil amintuḷḷaṉa. tamiḻarkku vēṇṭuvatu tamiḻppaṟṟu oṉṟē.
5
tamiḻum tiraviṭamum camamā?
kaṭanta mūvāyiram āṇṭukaḷākat tamiḻ maṟiyuṇṭu vantiruppata ṉālum, āriyak kulap piriviṉiyāl tamiḻum tamiḻarum tāḻttappaṭ ṭiruppataṉālum, āriya varukikku muṟpaṭṭa palvēṟu tuṟipaṟṟiya āyirak kaṇakkāṉa taṉittamiḻ nūlkaḷ aṉittum iyaṟkiyālum ceyaṟkiyālum iṟantupaṭṭamiyālum, moḻiyārāycci itupōtu cariyāṉa muṟiyil naṭi peṟāmiyālum, tamiḻik kāṭṭik koṭukkum kōṭarik kāmpukaḷ talimiyiṭam peṟṟiruppataṉālum, araciyalum palkalik kaḻakaṅkaḷum tamiḻ paṟṟiya uṇmikaḷi ārāyntaṟivatil akkaṟi koḷḷāmiyālum, tamiḻiṉ talimi iṉṉum tamiḻarālum cariyāy uṇarap peṟavilli.
tamiḻē tiraviṭattiṟku mūlam eṉpatu, palvēṟu cāṉṟukaḷāl teḷḷat teḷiya aṟiyak kiṭakkiṉṟatu. accāṉṟukaḷāvaṉa :-
1. varalāṟu kūṟal
tamiḻum ataṉ tiripāṉa tiraviṭamum, tamiḻarum avar vaḻiyiṉarāṉa tiraviṭarum, eṅku eṉṟu evvāṟu tōṉṟiṉar eṉṉum varalāṟṟuk kuṟippu tamiḻilaṉṟi ettiraviṭa moḻiyilumilli.
``paḵṟuḷi yāṟṟuṭaṉ paṉmali yaṭukkattuk
kumarik kōṭuṅ koṭuṅkaṭal koḷḷa
vaṭaticik kaṅkiyu mimayamuṅ koṇṭu
teṉṟici yāṇṭa teṉṉavaṉ vāḻi'' 	(cilap. 11 : 10-22)
``munnīr viḻaviṉ neṭiyōṉ
naṉṉīrp paḵṟuḷi maṇaliṉum palavē''	(puṟam. 9 : 10, 11)
``malitiri yūrntutaṉ maṇkaṭal vuvaliṉ
meliviṉṟi mēṟceṉṟu vēvārnā ṭiṭampaṭap
puliyoṭu viṉṉīkkip pukaḻpoṟitta kiḷarkeṇṭi
valiyiṉāṉ vaṇakkiya vāṭāccīrt teṉṉavaṉ''      (mullikkali, 4)
``.......... talic caṅkamiruntār ........... tamiḻārāyntatu kaṭal koḷḷap paṭṭa maturi yeṉpa.''
``.......... iṭic caṅkamiruntār ........... tamiḻārāyntatu kapāṭapuratteṉpa. akkālattup pōlum pāṇṭiyaṉāṭṭik kaṭal koṇṭatu.''	
(iṟiyaṉārakap poruḷ, 1, uri)
``mutalūḻi yiṟutikkaṇ teṉmaturi yakattut taliccaṅkattu .......... akkālattu avar nāṭṭut teṉpāli mukattiṟku vaṭavelliyākiya paḵṟuḷi yeṉṉumāṟṟiṟkum kumari yeṉṉu māṟṟiṟkumiṭiyē ēḻu nūṟṟuk kāvatavāṟum, ivaṟṟiṉ malivāṉeṉa malinta ......... nāṟpattoṉpatu nāṭum,  kumari kolla mutaliya paṉmali nāṭum kāṭum natiyum patiyum taṭanīrk kumari vaṭa peruṅ kōṭṭiṉkāṟum kaṭal koṇṭoḻitalāl''
- (cilap. 8 :1, aṭiyārkku nallār uri)
ikkuṟippukaḷ, tamiḻ allatu tamiḻar tōṉṟiya iṭam muḻukip pōṉa kumarik kaṇṭam eṉpatik kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. māntaṉ tōṉṟiya iṭamum kumarik kaṇṭamē. atu paṉimali (imayam) kaṭalukkuḷḷirunta kālattum kaḻi mutunilamāyirunta kaṇipparun toṉmi vāyntatu. aṭiyārkku nallār peyaruṭaṉ kuṟittuḷḷa ēḻēḻ nāṭum varalāṟ ṟuṇmiyē yaṉṟik kaṭṭuc ceytiyaṉṟu.
tiruvāṅkūr (tiruvitaṅkōṭu) nāṭṭil uḷḷa paṟaḷiyāṟē paṇṭit tamiḻilakkiyattiṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭa paḵṟuḷiyeṉpatu, tamiḻp pakivar kūṟṟeṉṟu viṭukka.
iṉi,
``centamiḻ cērnta paṉṉiru nilattum
taṅkuṟip piṉavē ticiccoṟ kiḷavi''	(tol. 883)
eṉṉum tolkāppiya nūṟpā, āriyattoṭu kalantu tiraviṭamāy māṟiya koṭun tamiḻt tōṟṟatti yuṇarttum.
2. āriyat toṭarpaṟṟa ilakkiyam
iṟantupaṭṭa mutu nāri, mutu kuruku, kaḷariyā viri, veṇṭāḷi, viyāḻamāli yakaval, ici nuṇukkam, ciṟṟici pērici, muṟuval, ceyiṟṟiyam, mativāṇar nāṭakat tamiḻ nūl mutaliya eṇṇiṟanta toṉṉūlkaḷum; ēraṇam (Logic), uruvam (sculpture), ūḻkam (yōkam), ici, nāṭakam, kaṇakku, poṉṉākkam, (Alchemy), kaṇkaṭṭi (cālam), vaciyam (Enchantment), cūṉiyam (Witchcraft), maṟam (pōr) mallam (Wrestling), yāṉi nūl, kutiri nūl, nīr nūl, nila nūl, poruḷ nūl (Economics), araciyal nūl (Politics), kaṭṭiṭa nūl (Architecture), ōviyam, kaṇiyam (Astrology) toṉ nūl (Astronomy), maṟi nūl (Scripture), mantira nūl (Mystif Philosophy), maruttuvam, maṭi nūl (Cookery) putiyal nūl mutaliya palvēṟu kaliyum aṟiviyalum paṟṟiya āyirak kaṇakkāṉa ariya nūlkaḷum; tolkāppiyattiṟ collappaṭṭuḷḷa, aṟuvakippāvum avaṟṟiṉ vēṟupāṭukaḷum,
``pāṭṭuri nūlē vāymoḻi piciyē
akaṅkatam mutucol''	(1236)
eṉṉum eḻunili yāppum, vāḻttuvakiyum paṇṇattiyum irupatu vaṇṇamum, eṇvaki vaṉappum nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa akapporuḷ puṟapporuṭṭuṟikaḷum ākiyavaṟṟiṟkuriya paṇṭi yilakkiyamum, āriyaccārpaṟṟa aruntamiḻ nūlkaḷē.
tiraviṭa moḻikaḷiluḷḷa ilakkiyamellām perumpālum vaṭamoḻi yitikāca purāṇaṅkaḷiṉ peyarppum tirippumē.
3. nūṟciṟappu
tolkāppiyam, tirukkuṟaḷ, kalittoki, cilappatikāram tirukkōvi, kampavirāmāyaṇam, tiruppukaḻ, kāḷamēka, māmpaḻak kavirāyar pāṭalkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟi yotta paṉuvalum pāṭalum et tiraviṭa moḻiyilum kāṇak
kiṭikkumō? āriyap pērilakkiya moḻikaḷilum avaṟṟik kāṇpatari
tāyirukkumpōtu, tiraviṭa moḻikaḷil avi illavē yilli yeṉpatic collavum vēṇṭumō?
4. ilakkaṇa amiti
mutalīṟi niliyeṉṉum eḻuttu variyaṟiyum, cila coṉmarapum, veṇpā, āciriyappā, kalippā, vañcippā eṉṉum yāppu vakiyum, akam puṟam eṉṉum poruṭpākupāṭum, centamiḻ eṉṉum varampum, tiraviṭa moḻikaṭkilli.
5. ilakkiyat toṉmi
talik kaḻakak kālattilēyē muttamiḻilakkaṇa vilakkiyam muṟṟiyiruntamiyāl, ki. mu. pattāyiram āṇṭukaṭkumuṉpē tamiḻilakkiyam tōṉṟiyiruntami pulaṉām. tiraviṭa moḻikaḷiluḷḷa ilakkiyamellām ki. pi. 8ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟkup piṟpaṭṭaviyē.
6. moḻittoṉmi
tamiḻt tōṟṟam ki. mu. ēṟattāḻa aimpatiṉāyiram āṇṭukaṭku muṟpaṭṭatu.
koṭuntamiḻkaḷuḷ, vaṭukeṉṉum teluṅku tirintatu. ki. mu. 10ām nūṟṟāṇṭu; karunaṭam eṉṉum kaṉṉaṭam tirintatu. ki. pi. 6ām nūṟṟāṇṭu; cēralam eṉṉum kēraḷam allatu maliyāḷam tirintatu. ki. pi. 13ām nūṟṟāṇṭu ēṉit tiraviṭaṅkaḷ tirintatu ivaṟṟiṟkiṭiyum piṉṉum.
7. moḻit tūymi
tamiḻ, pallāyirak kaṇakkāṉa ilakkiyac collum ulakiyaṟ collum iṟantupaṭṭuḷḷa ikkālattum, eḷḷaḷavum vaṭamoḻit tuṇiyiṉṟit taṉittu vaḻaṅkavum taḻittōṅkavum vallatu. tiraviṭa moḻikaṭkō vaṭamoḻit tuṇi iṉṟiyamiyātatu.
8. coṟkaḷiṉ iyaṉilimi
tamiḻc coṟkaḷ perumpālum muntiya iyaṉiliyilum tiraviṭac coṟkaḷ perumpālum pintiya tiriniliyilumē uḷḷaṉa. iti ōraricip patamākat teluṅkiṉiṉṟu eṭuttuk kāṭṭuvōm.
1. mūviṭap peyar
		tamiḻ	teluṅku
taṉmi	:	ēṉ-yāṉ-nāṉ	nēṉu
		ēm-yām	mēmu
		nām	maṉamu
muṉṉili	:	nīṉ-nī	nīvu
		nīm, nīyir-nīvir-nīr	mīru
paṭarkki	:	avaṉ	vāṭu
		avaḷ	āme
		avar	vāru
		atu, avaḷ	ati
		avi	avi
2. `āku' eṉṉum viṉippuṭi peyarcci
		tamiḻ	teluṅku
pakuti	:	ā, āku	avu
ēvalorumi	:	ā, āku	kā
ēvaṟpaṉmi	:	ākum, ākuṅkaḷ	kammu, kaṇṭi
(paṭarkki) āṇpāl
i. kā. muṟṟu	:	 āyiṉāṉ	ayiṉoṭu
i. kā. peyareccam	:	āṉa, āyiṉa	ayiṉa, aiṉa
i. ka. viṉiyeccam	:	āy, āki	ayi, ai
ni. nā. viṉiyeccam	:	āka	kā, ava
e. kā. viṉiyeccam	:	āyiṟṟēl	ayittē
(paṭarkki) oṉṟaṉ pāl
		tamiḻ	teluṅku
e. kā. muṟṟu	:	ākum - ām	avuṉu
uṭaṉpāṭṭiṭic col:	ām	avuṉu
oṉṟaṉ pāl
etirmaṟi muṟṟu	:	ākātu	kātu
toḻiṟ peyar	:	ātal, ākutal	avuṭṭa, kāvaṭamu
3. cila potuvakic coṟkaḷ
tamiḻ	teluṅku
curuṭṭu	cuṭṭa
tiruttu	tittu
paruppu	pappu
maruntu	mantu
9. colvaḷam
maliyāḷattil vaḻaṅkum vīṭu eṉṉum collum, kaṉṉaṭattil vaḻaṅkum maṉi eṉṉum collum. teluṅkil vaḻaṅkum il(lu) eṉṉum collum, tamiḻukkuriya oru poruṭ pala coṟkaḷām.
āy, iḷi, eḻili, kār, kāḷam, kuyiṉ, koṇṭal, koṇmu, cel, peyal, maṅkul, mañca, māl, mukil, mi eṉpaṉa mēkattik kuṟikkum vēṟupaṭṭa nuṇ poruṭ coṟkaḷām. ittakiya col vaḷattit tiraviṭa moḻikaḷilum kāṇaviyalātu.
10. muttamiḻp pākupāṭu
toṉṟu toṭṭu vaḻaṅkivarum iyalici nāṭakameṉṉum muttamiḻp pākupāṭum tiraviṭa moḻikaṭkilli.
pallāyiram āṇṭukaḷākap pāṇṭiya vēntar pōṟṟip puranta muttamiḻ, muttamiḻk kaḻaka nallicip pulavar, nalamuṟa nāṭiyāynta naṉmoḻi, piṟa moḻikaṭkillāta toṉmiyum muṉmiyum, teṉmiyum meṉmiyum, iḷamiyum vaḷamiyum eṇmiyum oṇmiyum, tāymiyum tūymiyum, cemmiyum, mummiyum, perumiyum, tirumiyum, mutumiyum, putumiyum tāṅki niṉṟum, tiraviṭa moḻikaḷuḷ oṉṟāka eḷivantum iḻivantum iruppatu ettuṇi iraṅkattakkatu!
``kaṇṇu taṟperuṅ kaṭavuḷuṅ kaḻakamō ṭamarntu
eṇṇu ṟatterin tāyntavip pacuntamiḻ ēṉi
maṇṇi ṭiccila ilakkaṇa varampilā moḻipōl
eṇṇi ṭippaṭak kiṭantatā eṇṇavum paṭumō''
eṉṉum parañcōti muṉivar pāṭaliyēṉuṅ kaṇṭu uṇmi teḷika.
6
`tiraviṭam' eṉpatē tītu
nāṭukaḷellām perumpālum moḻiyaṭippaṭiyilēyē amintiruppataṉālum, ataṉ vaḻiyākavē muṉṉēṟuvatālum, tamiḻar yāvarum tamiḻppaṟṟuk koṇṭiruttal vēṇṭum. ennāṭṭārāyiṉum, tamiḻppaṟṟuṭiyār tamiḻarē. innāṭṭārāyiṉum, aḵtillār ayalārē.
tamiḻp peyarē tāṅkutalum, iyaṉṟavari tūyatamiḻiṟ pēcutalum, eḻututalum, tirumaṇamum, caṭaṅkukaḷum kōyil vaḻipāṭum tamiḻilēyē naṭappittalum, makkaḷ ulakattil `tēvamoḻi'yilli yeṉa namputalum, intik kaṭṭāyak kalviyit tamiḻ nāṭṭil aṭiyōṭoḻittalum, tamiḻ nāṭṭiṟkut tamiḻ nāṭeṉṟa peyariṭutalum, tamiḻaṉ eṉpataṟku aṭiyāḷamām. tamiḻiṟku iṭik kālattil nērnta iḻiviṉāl pala teṉ coṟkaḷ vaḻakkaṟṟup pōyiṉa. avaṟṟi mīṇṭum vaḻakkiṟkuk koṇṭu varutal vēṇṭum.
kālṭuvel kaṇkāṇiyār mutaṉ muṟiyākat tirāviṭa moḻi kaḷi āyntatiṉālum, akkālattil tamiḻt tūymiyuṇarcciyiṉmi yālum, tamiḻit tiraviṭattiṉiṉṟu vēṟupaṭuttik kāṭṭat tēviyillā tiruntatu. ikkālattilō, ārāyccimikuntu viṭṭataṉālum, vaṭa moḻiyum intiyum paṟṟiya koḷkiyil, tamiḻarkkum piṟa iṉa moḻi yāḷarkkum vēṟupāṭiruppataṉālum, tamiḻeṉṟum, piṟiṉamoḻi kaḷiyē tiraviṭam eṉṟum vēṟu paṭuttik kāṭṭutal iṉṟiyamiyātatām. 
tamiḻ tūymiyāṉa teṉ moḻiyeṉṟum, tiraviṭam āriyaṅ kalanta teṉ moḻiyeṉṟum vēṟu pāṭaṟital vēṇṭum. pāl tayirāyt tirintapiṉ mīṇṭum pālākātatu pōl. vaṭamoḻi kalantu āriya vaṇṇamāyp pōṉa tiraviṭam mīṇṭum tamiḻākātu. vaṭamoḻik kalappāl tiraviṭam uyarum; tamiḻ tāḻum. ātalāl, vaṭa col cērac cērat tiraviṭattiṟku uyarvu; atu tīrattīrat tamiḻiṟku uyarvu. tiraviṭam eṉṟa moḻi niliyē vaṭamoḻik kalappāl tāṉ nērntatu. allākkāl atu koṭuntamiḻ eṉṟē paṇṭupōṟ kūṟappaṭum. tamiḻ taṉittiyaṅkum; tiraviṭam vaṭamoḻit tuṇiyiṉṟit tiṉittiyaṅkātu.
iṅṅaṉam, vaṭamoḻiyi naṭpākak koḷḷum tiraviṭattiṟkum pakiyākak koḷḷum tamiḻiṟkum, oru ciṟitum nērttam irukka muṭiyātu: ātalāl tamiḻ, tamiḻaṉ, tamiḻnāṭu eṉṟa coṟkaḷaṉṟit tiraviṭam. tiraviṭaṉ, tiraviṭanāṭu eṉṟa coṟkaḷ olittal kūṭātu tiraviṭam ariyāriyamum mukkālāriyamumātalāl, ataṉōṭu tamiḻi iṇippiṉ, aḻukaloṭu cērnta naṟkaṉiyum keṭuvatupōl keṭṭuppōm. piṉpu tamiḻumirātu, tamiḻaṉumirāṉ, intiyā muḻutum āriyamāy viṭum.
tamiḻ, tiraviṭamoḻikaḷippōla āriyac cārpu koḷḷāmi yālum, vaṭa moḻiyiyum intiyiyum ēṟkātatālum, tamiḻ nāṭṭiṟku moḻiyiyal taṉṉāṭci (Lingusitic Autonomy) peṟutal vēṇṭum. pōkkuvarattu, taṟkāppu, veḷināṭṭuṟavu ākiya muttuṟiyilum, intiyak kūṭṭaracu aṭaṅkiyirukkalām.

7
moḻi peyar muṟi
oru moḻiyiluḷḷa coṟkaḷi allatu kuṟiyīṭukaḷi vēṟu moḻiyil peyarkkum muṟikaḷ aintām. aviyāvaṉa :-
1. nērccol (e-kā : pen - tūval)
2. vērp poruṭ col. (e-kā : motor - iyaṅki)
3. ciṟappiyaṟ col. (e-kā : trin - puki vaṇṭi)
4. olippolic col
5. mutaleḻuttuc col
ivaṟṟuḷ, mutalatu muṉṉamēyuḷḷa col. ēṉiyavellām putitāy ākkap peṟuvaṉa.
ï›it tifíŸ, Kjš _‹W« ``jÄœ jÅ¤âa§Fkh?'' eṉṉum muntiya kaṭṭuriyiṟ kūṟappaṭṭuḷa. ēṉiyiraṇṭē iṅkuk kūṟappeṟum.
oppolic collāvatu, oliyilum poruḷilum okkumāṟu amittuk koḷḷap peṟum col.
e-kā :	āṅkilam	tamiḻ
	Parliament	pārāḷumaṉṟu
	bracket	piṟikkōṭu
Parliament eṉpatu pēcuṅkaḷam eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum taṉic col (oru col). pārāḷumaṉṟu eṉpatu pāri āḷum maṉṟu eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum muccoṟṟoṭar.
Parler eṉpatu pēcu eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum pireñcu viṉic col; ment eṉpatu oru pireñcut toḻiṟ peyarīṟu.
ākavē, ivviru coṟkaṭkum eḷḷaḷavum toṭarpilli āyiṉum, oliyilum pēraḷavu ottirukkiṉṟaṉa.
Braccal eṉpatu breeches (kuṟuṅkāṟ caṭṭikaḷ) eṉṟu poruḷ paṭum ilattīṉ col. Braga eṉpatu ataṉ icuppāṉiyat (Spanish) tiripu. Bragueta eṉpatu ataṉ kuṟumip poruḷ vaṭivam (Diminutive). ataṉiṉṟu mutalil bragget eṉṟum, āṅkilac col tirintatu.
piṟik kōṭu eṉpatu piṟipōl vaḷintuḷḷa kōṭu eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum iru coṟṟoṭar.
ivaṟṟiṟkum oru toṭarpumilli. āyiṉum, oliyum poruḷum ottuḷḷaṉa. iṅṅaṉam amital mika arumiyātaliṉ, oru cila coṟkaḷ tām im muṟiyil amikkappeṟum.
mutaleḻuttuc collāvatu, oru poruḷip paṟṟiya vaṇṇaṉic coṟkaḷ mutaleḻuttukkaḷimaṭṭum cērttu oru peyar allatu kuṟiyīṭu amittal.
e. kā : Radar.
itu, radio detection and ranging eṉṉum nāṟcol mutaleḻuttuk kōppu. immuṟi paṟṟiyē, katiriyattuppaṟivum vīccīṭum eṉṉum muṟcoṟkaḷiṉ mutaleḻuttukkaḷic cērttuk katuvī eṉṟō, (vāṉūrti marakkalam kaṭaṟkari mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ) vākkum (direction) vīccum (range) kāṇi eṉṉum poruḷil vāvīṇi eṉṟō, amittuk koḷḷalām.
āṅkilak kuṟiyīṭukaḷellām poruḷaḷavil peruvaḷaṅ koṇṭaṉa vēṉum, collaḷavil mika eḷiya muṟiyilēyē amin tuḷḷaṉa. mēṟkūṟiya aimmuṟikaḷiyum kiyāḷiṉ ellā aṟiviyaṟ kuṟiyīṭukaḷiyum tamiḻil eḷitāy moḻi peyarttu viṭalām. tāy moḻip paṟṟoṉṟē tēvi.
8
nikaḻkāla viṉi vaṭivam
tolkāppiyar,
``viṉiyeṉap paṭuvatu vēṟṟumi koḷḷātu
niṉiyuṅ kālik kālamoṭu tōṉṟum''	(viṉiyiyal. 1)
``kālan tāmē mūṉṟeṉa moḻipa''	(viṉiyiyal. 2)
``iṟappi ṉikaḻvi ṉetirvi ṉeṉṟā
ammuk kālamuṅ kuṟippoṭuṅ koḷḷu
meynnili yuṭiya tōṉṟa lāṟē''	(viṉiyiyal. 3)
eṉṉum nūṟpākkaḷ vāyilāka viṉic coṟkālam mūṉṟeṉak kūṟiyiruppiṉum nikaḻkālaviṉi muṟṟuvaṭivilō eccavaṭivilō iṭanilikkāṭṭi yāṇṭuṅkūṟavēyilli. avar,
``ceytu ceyyūc ceypu ceyteṉac
ceyyiyar ceyyiya ceyiṉceyac ceyaṟkeṉa
avavaki oṉpatum viṉiyeñcu kiḷavi''
eṉa viṉiyeccavāypāṭukaḷi, oruvāṟu nikaḻkālamum aṭaṅka mukkālattiṟkum tokuttuk kūṟiṉōrāṉum, peyarecca vāypāṭukaḷik kūṟumiṭattu.
``ceyyum ceyta eṉṉuñ collē''
eṉa, nikaḻkālattaṟṟum etirkālattiṟkum potuppaṭac `ceyyum', eṉṉum orē vāypāṭṭi āṇṭārēyaṉṟi,
``ceyta ceykiṉṟa ceyyum eṉ pāṭṭiṉ''
eṉa naṉṉūlārip pōla nikaḻ kālattiṟkeṉat taṉippaṭa oṉṟiyuṅ kūṟiṟṟilar.
cēṉāvariyar uḷḷiṭṭa uriyāciriyaṉmārum, mukkāla viṉi muṟṟiṟkum ``uṇṭāṉ, uṇṇā niṉṟāṉ, uṇpāṉ'' eṉa eṭuttuk kāṭṭiṉarēṉum nikaḻkāla viṉiyit tolkāppiyar vitantu kuṟippiṭum nūṟpākkaḷiṉkīḻ ceyyā niṉṟāṉ eṉṉum vāypāṭṭu viṉimuṟṟik kāṭṭāviṭattellām `ceyyum' eṉṉum vāypāṭṭu viṉiyiyē eṭuttāṇṭuḷḷaṉar. `āniṉṟu' eṉṉum iṭinili `kiṉṟu' eṉpatupōl attuṇic ciṟantataṉṟu. āyiṉum, atuvum tolkāppiyattiṟ kāṇappeṟavilli.
kiṟu kiṉṟu āniṉṟu eṉa nikaḻkāla iṭinili mūṉṟeṉpar naṉṉūlār. ivaṟṟuḷ kiṟu eṉpatu kiṉṟu eṉpataṉ tokuttalē. ivaṟṟi iraṇṭākak koḷḷiṉum, mūṉṟākak koḷḷiṉum, iḻukkaṉṟām, āyiṉ ivaṟṟuḷ oṉṟik kūṭat tolkāppiyar taṉiyākavē viṉiccollil vittō kuṟiyātatu mikavum viyattaṟkuriyatē.
iṉinikaḻkāla viṉipaṟṟiya tolkāppiya nūṟpākkaḷum,
avaṟṟiṟkuc cēṉāvarayar tanta eṭuttukkāṭṭum, varumāṟu :-
``nikaḻuu niṉṟa palarvari kiḷaviyiṉ
uyartiṇiyorumi tōṉṟalu murittē
aṉṉa marapiṉ viṉivayi ṉāṉa''	(peyariyal 3.19)
e-kā : cāttaṉyāḻeḻuum, cātti cāntarikkum,
``pallōr paṭarkki muṉṉili taṉmi
avvayiṉ mūṉṟum nikaḻuṅ kālattuc
ceyyum eṉṉum kiḷaviyoṭu koḷḷā''	(viṉiyiyal 30)
(itaṟku eṭuttukkāṭṭu nūṟpāvilēyē uḷḷatu)
``nilaṉum poruḷuṅ kālamuṅ karuviyum
viṉimutaṟ kiḷaviyum viṉiyu muḷappaṭa
avvaṟu poruṭkumō raṉṉa vurimiya
ceyyuñ ceyta veṉṉuñ collē.''	(viṉiyiyal 37)
e-kā :	vāḻumil, kaṟkunūl, tuyiluṅkālam, vaṉiyuṅkōl, otum pārppāṉ, uṇṇumūṉ...... (pukkavil, uṇṭa cōṟu, vantanāḷ, veṉṟavēl, āṭiyakūttaṉ, pōyiṉa pōkku)
``avaṟṟoṭu varuvaḻic ceyyumeṉ kiḷavi
mutaṟkaṇ varinta mūviṟṟu murittē''	(viṉiyiyal 38)
(itaṟkum eṭuttukkāṭṭu nūṟpāvilēyē uḷḷatu)
``munnilik kālamun tōṉṟu miyaṟki
emmuṟic collu nikaḻuṅ kālattu
meynnilip potuccoṟ kiḷattal vēṇṭum''       (viṉiyiyal 43)
e-kā :
``maliniṟku, ñāyiṟiyaṅkum, tiṅkaḷiyaṅkum eṉavum'' ``tavaṅkatirk kaṉaliyoṭu mativalan tiritirun taṇkaṭal viyattu'' eṉavum varum
``mikkataṉ maruṅkiṉ viṉiccoṟ cuṭṭi

ceyva tilvaḻi nikaḻuṅ kālattu
meypeṟat tōṉṟum poruṭṭā kummē''	(viṉiyiyal 45)
e-kā : 	........ oruvaṉ ṟavañceyyiṟ cuvarkkam pukum, tāyik kolliṉ nirayam pukum eṉavum, mikka taṉviṉic coṉōkki ammikkataṉṟiriyil paṇpu kuṟitta viṉi mutaṟ kiḷavi nikaḻkālattāṉ vantavāṟu kaṇṭukoḷka.
``vārāk kālattu viṉiccoṟ kiḷavi
iṟappiṉu nikaḻviṉuñ ciṟappat tōṉṟum
iyaṟkiyun teḷivuṅ kiḷakkuṅ kāli''	(viṉiyiyal 48)
e-kā :
``....... kūṟi kōṭpaṭā muṉṉum, ikkāṭṭuṭ pōkiṟ
kūṟi kōṭpaṭṭāṉ kūṟi kōṭpaṭum eṉṉum ...........
maḻipeyyāmuṉṉum, maḻi peytatu, maḻi peyyum eṉṉum āṇṭu etirkālattiṟkuriya poruḷ iṟanta kālattāṉum nikaḻkālat tāṉum tōṉṟiyavāṟu kaṇṭukoḷka.
itukāṟum kūṟiyavaṟṟāl, tolkāppiyar `ceyyum' eṉṉum etirkālaviṉi vāypāṭṭiyē, nikaḻkālattiṟkuḷ koṇṭami teḷiyalām. āyiṉ avar kālattuc `ceykiṉṟāṉ' eṉṉum vāypāṭṭu viṉi vaḻakkil ilatōveṉiṉ uṇṭu, ataṉi avar cevviyāy ārāyntilar eṉṟē koḷḷalvēṇṭum. aḵteṅṅaṉameṉiṉ, kūṟuval.
`ceykiṉṟu' eṉṉum pālīṟaṟṟa nikaḻkāla viṉi, paḻañcēra nāṭākiya malayāḷa nāṭṭil, ceyyuntu eṉat tirintu iruvaki vaḻakkilum vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu it tiripiṉpaṭiyē, ceykiṉṟāṉ eṉṉum pālīṟēṟṟaṟa vaṭivam, muṟiyē, ceykunnāṉ - ceykunaṉ eṉṟum ceykunnāṉ - ceyyunnāṉ - ceyyunaṉ - ceynaṉ eṉṟum tirintu, tamiḻilakkiyattil toṉṟutoṭṭu vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu. iṅṅaṉamē ceykunar ceynar eṉap palarpāṟ collum ceykuna. ceyna eṉap palaviṉpāṟ collum, vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉa. iviyellām tamiḻil nikaḻkālaṅkuṟitta viṉiyā laṇiyum peyarkaḷām. makiḻnaṉ, vāḻnaṉ (vāṇaṉ) mutaliyaṉavum ittakiyaṉavē. ceyyuntu eṉṉum maliyāḷa vaṭivam nikaḻkāla viṉimuṟṟām.
`ceykuna' `ceyna' eṉṉum vāypāṭṭup palaviṉpāl viṉiyālaṇiyum peyarkaḷ, tolkāppiyam palaviṭattum payiṉṟu varukiṉṟaḷa.
e-kā : eḻuttatikāram (1) tokimarapu
``iyalpā kunavum uṟaḻā kunavum''	(9)
``iyalpā kunavum valleḻuttu mikunavum
uṟaḻā kunavum.''	(16)
``valleḻuttu mikunavum uṟaḻā kunavum''	(17)
(2) kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇariyal.
``kiḷanta valla ceyyuḷuḷ tirinavum
vaḻaṅkiyal maruṅkil maruvoṭu tirinavum''	(78)
collatikāram (1) iṭiyiyal.

acinilik kiḷavi āki varunavum
tattam kuṟippiṟ poruḷ ceykunavum
oppil vaḻiyāṟ poruḷ ceykunavum''	(2)
(2) eccaviyal
``peyar nilik kiḷaviyiṉ āakunavum

mantirap poruḷvayiṉ āakunavum''	(53)

``aṉpuṟu takuna iṟicciyuṭ cuṭṭalum''	(37)
iḵtēl, tolkāppiyar tam nūluḷ tām kūṟiyatiyē ēṉ ārāyntilar eṉiṉ.
``toṇṭu taliyiṭṭa pattukkuṟi eḻunūṟṟu''	(100)
eṉa avar ceyyuḷiyalil tām kūṟiyatiyum nōkkātē.
``oṉpāṉ okaramicit takaram oṟṟum
munti oṟṟē ṇakāram iraṭṭum
paḵteṉ kiḷavi āytapaka raṅkeṭa
niṟṟal vēṇṭum ūkārak kiḷavi
oṟṟiya takaram ṟakara mākum''
eṉak kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇariyaliṟ kūṟiyatupōṉṟē eṉakkūṟi viṭukka (toṇṭu 9. toṇṭu pattu - toṇpatu (90) toṉpatu - oṉpatu. toṇṭu nūṟu toṇṇūṟu (90) toṇṭu āyiram toḷḷāyiram 900).
``aṟṟēl kiṉṟu eṉpatu ṟaṉṉakaraṅ koṇṭirukkavum ataṉ tirivil tannakaram vanta teṅṅaṉameṉiṉ tantu - tantu, vannu - vannu eṉat tantanakaram payiṉṟuvarum cēranāṭṭu vaḻakkuppiṉpaṟṟi yeṉka. ceynaṉ eṉṉum vaṭivu tōṉṟiyatē mūkkolimikkup piyalum cēranāṭṭiltāṉ.
itukāṟum kūṟivaṟṟāl, `kiṉṟu' eṉṉum nikaḻkāla iṭinili tirintō tiriyātō tolkāppiyar kālattilum vaḻaṅkiṟṟeṉṟum, avar nikaḻkālattiṟkut taṉi viṉi vaṭivu kūṟātatu kuṉṟak kūṟalāmeṉṟum aṟintukoḷka.

9
nikaḻkāla viṉiyeccam ētu?
``ceytu ceypu ceyyac ceyyūc
ceyteṉac ceyacceyiṉ ceyyiya ceyyiyar
vāṉpāṉ pākkiṉa viṉiyeccampiṟa
aintoṉṟā ṟumuk kālamu muṟittarum''	(343)
eṉṉum nūṟpāvāl `ceyya' eṉṉum vāppāṭṭu viṉiyeccam allatu `ceya' eṉṉum ataṉ tokuttal vaṭivu, nikaḻkālamuṇarntum eṉak kūṟiṉar pavaṇanti muṉivar. itiyoṭṭiyē, `ceyya' eṉpatu nikaḻkāla viṉiyecca vāypāṭeṉṟu toṉṟutoṭṭu vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu. āyiṉ tolkāppiyar iṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟāmi maṭṭumiṉṟi
``ceytu ceyyūc ceypu ceyteṉac
ceyyiyar ceyyiya ceyiṉceyac ceyaṟkeṉa
avvaki oṉpatum viṉiyeñcu kiḷavi.''	(713)
eṉa `ceya' eṉṉum vāypāṭṭi etirkāla viṉiyeccam eṉak karutumāṟu, `ceyyiyar' `ceyyiya' `ceyiṉ' eṉṉum etirkāla viṉiyecca vāypāṭukaṭkum, `ceyaṟkeṉa' eṉṉum etirkāla viṉiyecca vāypāṭṭiṟkum iṭiyil niṟuttiyumuḷḷār.
iṉi, `ceymmaṉa' eṉṉum vāppāṭōveṉiṉ, atuvum etirkāla viṉiyeccamē. `ceymmār' eṉṉum vāypāṭu `vāṉ' pār īṟṟu viṉiyeccaṅkaḷiṉ paṉmiyāṉa muṟṟeccamāyt teritaliṉ, atuvum etirkālac colleṉa aṟiyappaṭum.
tolkāppiyar,
``viṉiyeṉap paṭuvatu vēṟṟumi koḷḷātu
niṉiyuṅ kālik kālamoṭu tōṉṟum''	(683)
``kālat tāmē mūṉṟeṉa moḻipa''	(684)
eṉa muṟṟum eccamumākiya iruvaki viṉiccoṟkum potuvākak kūṟiyiruttaliṉ nikaḻkāla viṉiyeccamum tamiḻkkuṇṭeṉpatu tēṟṟam.
toṉṟutoṭṭu nikaḻkāla viṉiyeccameṉa vaḻaṅki varum `ceyya' eṉṉum vāypāṭṭuc col, kāraṇa kāriyamum uṭaṉikaḻcciyum kāriya kāraṇamum paṟṟivarum toṭarkaḷil muṟiyē mukkālamum uṇarttutaliṉ. nikaḻkālattiṟkuc ciṟappupā vuriyateṉak koḷḷutal poruntātu.
e-kā :
maḻipeyyappayir viḷintatu - kāraṇa kāriyam (iṟantakālam)
miṉṉal miṉṉa iṭi iṭikkiṟatu - uṭaṉikaḻcci (nikaḻkālam)
payir viḷiya maḻippeyyum - kāriya karaṇam (etirkālam)
iṉi, uṭaṉikaḻccipaṟṟi, `ceyya' eṉṉum viṉiyecca vāy pāṭṭi nikaḻkālameṉak koḷḷutumeṉiṉ, atuvum mukkālattiṟkum potuvāy varutaliṉ, aṅṅaṉam kōṭaṟkuriyataṉṟām.
e-kā :
cāttaṉ colla iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ kēṭṭaṉar - i. kā.
kaṇṇoṉṟu kāṇak karuttoṉṟu nāṭukiṉṟatu - ni. kā.
murukaṉ muḻaviyakkak koṟṟaṉ kuḻalūtuvāṉ - e. kā.
iṅṅaṉam `ceyya' eṉṉum vāypāṭṭu viṉiyeccam oru vakiyilum nikaḻkālattic ciṟappāy uṇarttāmiyiṉ, ataṉiṉ vēṟāṉa oru collē uṇmi nikaḻkāla viṉiyeccamāyiruttal vēṇṭum. `ceyya' eṉṉum nikaḻkāla viṉiyeccattiṟku nērāṉa `to do Infinitive , Mood)' eṉṉum āṅkila viṉivaṭivamum etirkālattiyē uṇarttuvatum, ceyya eṉṉum vaṭivuccol iyalpāka iṉimēṟ ceyya eṉṟē poruḷpaṭuvatum iṅkuk kavaṉikkattakkaṉa.
āṅkilattil nikaḻkāla viṉiyeccam (present , participle) eṉṟu collap paṭuvatu `doing' eṉṉum vāypāṭṭu viṉiccollākum. ataṟku nērāṉa tamiḻ vāypāṭu `ceytukoṇṭu' eṉpatē. `taccaṉ peṭṭi ceytu koṇṭu irukkiṉṟāṉ', eṉṉum coṟṟoṭaril, `ceytukoṇṭu' eṉṉum col irucoṟpōl tōṉṟiṉum oru coṟṟaṉmippaṭṭum, eccap poruḷ koṇṭum nikaḻkālattic ciṟappāy uṇarttiyum, niṟṟal kāṇka. `ceytu koṇṭu' eṉṉum vāypāṭṭu viṉiyeccam, iruntāṉ irukkiṉṟāṉ iruppāṉ eṉṉum mukkāla muṟṟoṭum poruntutaliṉ, nikaḻkālattiṟkuc ciṟappāy urittātal eṅṅaṉameṉiṉ, `ceytu' eṉṉum iṟantakāla viṉiyeccam ceytu vantāṉ, ceytu varukiṉṟāṉ, ceytu varuvāṉ eṉa mukkāla muṟṟoṭum poruntumāyiṉum iṟanta kālattiṟkē ciṟappāyuriyateṉṟu koḷḷappaṭṭāṟpōṉṟē `ceytu koṇṭu' eṉṉum nikaḻkāla viṉiyeccamum mukkāla muṟṟoṭum poruntumāyiṉum, nikaḻkālattiṟkē ciṟappāyuriyateṉṟu koḷḷap paṭṭateṉka.
aṟṟēl, `ceytu koṇṭu' eṉṉum vāypāṭṭuc col tolkāppiyattum caṅka nūlkaḷilum kāṇappaṭāmiyiṉ piṟkālattuc colleṉṟu koḷḷappaṭumeṉiṉ aṟṟaṉṟu, ulaka vaḻakkum ceyyuḷ vaḻakkum eṉac coṟkaḷ iru tokutippaṭṭu niṟṟaliṉ, nīṉ eṉṉum muṉṉiliyorumip peyar iṟṟit teṉpāṇṭi nāṭṭilum karunaṭanāṭṭilum toṉṟutoṭṭu ulaka vaḻakkāy vaḻaṅkivaravum ataṉ kaṭik kuṟiyāṉa nī eṉṉum collē ilakkaṇa vilakkiyaṅkaḷiṟ payiṉṟu varutalpōl, `ceytu koṇṭu' eṉṉum collum toṉṟu toṭṭu ulaka vaḻakkil iruntuvariṉum ataṟkīṭāka ceytu eṉṉum iṟantakāla viṉiyeccamum `ceytaṉaṉ' eṉṉum muṟṟeccamumē `ceytu koṇṭu' eṉṉum nikaḻkāla viṉiyeccap poruḷil ilakkiya vaḻakkāy iruntu varukiṉṟaṉa eṉka.
e-kā :
pāṭi vantāṉ, pāṭiṉaṉ vantāṉ	-	i. kā.
pāṭi varukiṉṟāṉ pāṭiṉaṉ varukiṉṟāṉ	-	ti. kā.
pāṭi varuvāṉ pāṭiṉaṉ varuvāṉ	-	e. kā.
ivaṟṟil , pāṭi eṉpatu pāṭikkoṇṭu eṉṟu poruḷpaṭutal kāṇka.
ilakkaṇavilakkiya nūlkaḷ ettuṇip parantupaṭṭaṉavāyiṉum akara varicic coṟkaḷañciyaṅkaḷallaveṉṟum `ceytu koṇṭu' eṉṉum viṉiyecca vāypāṭu caṅkakāla ulakavaḻakkilli yeṉpataṟku yātoru cāṉṟumilliyeṉṟum, aṟital vēṇṭum.
puliyāṟ kollappaṭṭa marutaṉ eṉṟum, pēyāṟ piṭikkappaṭṭa nākaṉ eṉṟum collaṟkēṟṟa ceyappāṭṭu viṉi tamiḻiliruntum, pulikoṉṟa marutaṉ, pēypiṭitta nākaṉ eṉpaṉavē iyalpāṉa iruvaki vaḻakkumātalāl, cila ilakkaṇac col vaṭivaṅkaḷ muṟṟāṭci peṟṟiruppa toṉṟē ataṉ maṟuvaṭivi maṟukkuñ cāṉṟākāteṉpatit teṟṟeṉa vuṇarka.
iṉi teluṅkil `ceytu koṇṭu' eṉṉum eccattiṟku nērāṉa `cēcukoṉi' eṉṉum vaṭivattuṭaṉ, `cēttu' eṉa maṟṟoru nikaḻkāla viṉiyecca vaṭivum iruppatu makiḻattakka toṉṟākum.

10
kālṭuvel kaṇkāṇiyāriṉ caṟukkalkaḷ - tamiḻ vēṟṟumiyamippu
emakkut terintavari, tamiḻilum tamiḻippaṟṟiyum itukāṟum eḻutiyuḷḷavarāy itupōtu aṟiyappaṭum nūlāciriyaruḷ, tol kāppiyar, tiruvaḷḷuvar, kālṭuvel, maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ eṉṉum nālvarē nāyakamāṇavar. ivaruḷ ovvoruvarum ovvoru tuṟiyil oppuyarvaṟṟa taṉippērāṟṟalar āyiṉum, mūvaki nūliṉum mutaṉūlē ciṟantatu eṉṉum muṟimi nōkkiṉ, tiruvaḷḷuvarum kālṭuvelum ēṉiyiruvariṉum tikaḻntu tōṉṟuvar. 
ilakkaṇattilum vēṟāka moḻi nūl eṉa oru taṉinūl aṟiyap
paṭātu. nāvalantēya moḻikaḷellām tēvamoḻiyāṉa camaṟkirutattiṉ tiripeṉṟu perumpāl tamiḻarāluṅ koḷḷappaṭṭirunta kālattil, tiraviṭa moḻi ittuṇi eṉa vakuttuk kāṭṭiyum avi āriyattilum vēṟeṉap pirittuk kāṭṭiyum, tiraviṭa moḻittuṟit tiṇṇiruḷip pōkkiya mēlik katiravaṉ kālṭuvel kaṇkāṇiyā rāvar. āyiṉum, ``āṇikkum aṭi caṟukkum'' ātaliṉ pirāmaṇa varukikku muntiya paḻan
tamiḻ nūlkaḷellām iṟantupaṭṭu, āyirakkaṇakkāṉa tūya teṉ coṟkaḷ aḻivuṟṟu, palvēṟu tuṟiyilum tamiḻar tāḻvuṇṭu, tolkāppiyam uḷḷiṭṭa caṅkanūṟpayiṟci kuṉṟi kumari nāṭṭuṇmi maṟintirunta peruvaḻukkalil vaḻikāṭṭiyiṉṟic cella vēṇṭiyiruntatāl, iraṇṭōr iṭattil caṟukkiviḻa nērntatu. eṉiṉum atu eṭṭuṇiyum avaratu aruntoṇṭiṉ perumiyik kuṟikkātu. tavaṟuvatu māntaṉ iyalpē, ārāycciyillāta māṇavariṉ aiyamaṟuttaṟporuṭṭē, avaruṭiya caṟukkalkaḷ iṅku eṭuttuk kāṭṭappeṟukiṉṟaṉa eṉka.
kāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷa kāraṇampaṟṟi, tiraviṭattiluḷḷa ilakkaṇa muḷḷiṭṭa ellā uyar nūlkaḷum kalikaḷum āriya moḻiyiṉam eṉa tavaṟāṉa muṉ muṭipukoṇṭuviṭṭatiṉālēyē, tamiḻ ilakkaṇa vēṟṟumiyamippu vaṭamoḻiyilakkaṇa vēṟṟumiyamippit taḻuviyateṉap piṉvarumāṟu tavaṟāka varintuḷḷār kālṭuvel kaṇkāṇiyār.
``tiraviṭa moḻikaḷiṉ vēṟṟumiyamippiṉ kūṟupāṭṭi āyappukuṅkāl tiraviṭa ilakkaṇiyar avaṟṟi oḻuṅkupaṭuttiyuḷḷa muṟiyiyē kiyāḷvōm. atu camaṟkiruta muṟiyē. ivvakiyil camaṟkirutattip piṉpaṟṟiyatu uṇmiyil vaḻuvē. ēṉeṉiṉ, camaṟkirutattil eṭṭē vēṟṟumiyirukka tamiḻ teluṅku mutaliyavaṟṟi luḷḷa vēṟṟumi yeṇṇikki perumpālum tiṭṭamaṟṟatāyiruk kiṉṟatu. oru peyariṉ īṟṟil cērkkappeṟum ovvoru piṉṉoṭṭum, cariyāyc coṉṉāl, oru putu vēṟṟumiyākiṉṟatu. ataṉāl attakiya vēṟṟumikaḷiṉ eṇṇikki, pēcuvōṉiṉ tēviyiyum vaṇ veḷippaṭutta virumpum palvēṟu nuṇporuḷ vēṟupāṭṭiyum poṟut
tuḷḷatu. vitappāka, cila vēḷikaḷil vēṟṟumit tiripu eṉa amikkap
peṟum vēṟṟumiyaṭi, cilaviṭattukkiḻamip poruḷum cilaviṭattu iṭapporuḷum, cilaviṭattu peyareccap poruḷum taruvatāl atu oru taṉiyiṭam peṟṟiruttal vēṇṭum. uṭaṉikaḻcci vēṟṟumiyum aṅṅaṉamē. (vēṟṟumiyaṭiyiyuṅ karuvi vēṟṟumiyi yum kāṇka) iṅṅaṉamiruntum, tiraviṭa ilakkaṇiyar vaḻakkam vēṟṟumi yeṇṇikkiyi eṭṭāka variyaṟuttirukkiṉṟatu. ivvoḻuṅku pāṭṭiṟ pala vacatik kuṟi
kaḷiruppiṉum nām pukavirukkum kūṟupāṭṭāy vil potuvāṉa vaḻakkit taḻuvuvatu teḷivuṇarccik kēṟṟatāyirukkum. tamiḻ ilakkaṇiyar camaṟkiruta vēṟṟumiyoḻuṅkip piṉpaṟṟuṅ kāl, koṭi vēṟṟumi nīkka
vēṟṟumi eṉap poruḷpaṟṟip peyariṭāmal eṇpaṟṟiyē camaṟkiruta muṟiyittaḻuvi peyariṭṭarukkiṉṟaṉar. avar, camaṟkirutattiluḷḷa varicippaṭiyē, mutal vēṟṟumi iraṇṭām vēṟṟumi eṉa eṭṭām vēṟṟumi variyum, ovvoru vēṟṟumik kum muṉ ōr eṇṇi
yiṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉar. eḻuvāy vēṟṟumi allatu mutal vēṟṟumi tiraviṭa vēṟṟumi variciyiṉ talippil niṉṟālum. immoḻikaḷāl āḷappeṟum uṇmiyāṉa vēṟṟumikaḷ tirivēṟṟumikaḷē''.
itaṉ maṟuppu varumāṟu:-
``vēṟṟumi tāmē ēḻeṉa moḻipa''	(tol. 546)
``viḷikoḷ vataṉkaṇ viḷiyōṭeṭṭē''	(tol. 547)
eṉat tolkāppiyam poruḷpaṟṟi vēṟṟumi eṭṭēyeṉat tiṭṭamāy variyaṟuttuk kūṟuvatāl, tiraviṭa vēṟṟumi yeṇṇikki tiṭṭamaṟṟatu eṉak kūṟuvatu poruntātu. tolkāppiyattiṉ vaḻi nūlāṉa naṉṉūlum,
``ēṟku mevvakip peyarkkum ṟāypporuḷ
vēṟṟumi ceyvaṉa eṭṭē vēṟṟumi'' eṉa	(naṉ. 291)
variyaṟiyē kāraṅkoṭuttuk kūṟutal kāṇka.

eṉa oṭuccol iruporuḷilum varutal kāṇka.		
iṉi 5 vēṟṟumip poruḷkaḷākiya nīṅkal oppu elli ētu eṉṉum nāṉkaṉuḷ, iṟuti mūṉṟum mutalataṉ nuṇ vēṟupāṭukaḷē.
oppu eṉpatu uvamapporuvu uṟaḻporuvu eṉa irutiṟappaṭiṉum ``kākkiyiṟ karitu kaḷampaḻam'' eṉpatu 5ām vēṟṟumiyāyp pala kākkiyiviṭak karitu eṉṟē poruḷpaṭum. kākkiyippōlak karitu eṉṉumporuḷ vēṟṟumik kuriyatām. ``kipporuḷ taṉṉiṉ meypporuḷkalvi'' eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa toṭarkaḷellām uṟaḻ poruvuṟṟu 5ām vēṟṟumikkēyuriyaṉavām. oru vakiyil oppumi koṇṭa iruporuḷkaḷuḷ, oṉṟu uṟaḻntu allatu viñci niṟpiṉ oppumiyiṉiṉṟu nīṅkutal kāṇka.
iṉi `tilliyiṉ vaṭakku ceṉṉi' eṉpatil iṭavakiyāṉa nīṅkaṟporuḷum, vāṇikattiṉ āyiṉāṉ vaṭivēl eṉpatil nili vakiyāl nīṅkaṟ poruḷum iruttal kāṇka. iṅṅaṉam 5ām vēṟṟumip poruḷkaḷellām nīkkap poruḷēyātal nōkkitteḷika.
ēṉi vēṟṟumikaḷellām ovvoru poruḷē koṇṭaṉa veṉpatu ellārkkum teḷivām.

eṉap piṟpaṭṭa nūṟpākkaḷilum muṟkūṟiya muṟiyiyē mutaṉmiyākak kiyāḷutal kāṇka. naṉṉūlārum tolkāppiyari aṭiyoṟṟiyē vēṟṟumikaḷip peyariṭṭaḻikkiṉṟār.
eḻuvāy eṉiṉum peyar eṉiṉum okkum.
iṅṅaṉam poruḷi aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭu urupupaṟṟi vēṟṟumikaḷi varicippaṭuttik kūṟumpōtē, eṇ muṟi paṟṟip peyariṭṭāḷutaṟkum tōṟṟuvāy ceytuḷḷatu. tolkāppiyam allatu ataṉ muṉṉūlāṉa toṉṉūl. ākavē, eṇ muṟipaṟṟi vēṟṟumi kaḷip peyariṭṭāḷvatu tamiḻil talimiyāṉataṉṟeṉpatum peṟṟām.
camaṟkiruta ilakkaṇam tamiḻilakkaṇattiṟkup piṟpaṭṭataṉālum ataṉ vaḻipaṭṭatiṉālum, tamiḻmuṟiyip piṉpaṟṟi, piṉvarumāṟu vēṟṟumikaḷi vakuttuk kūṟukiṉṟatu.
	vēṟṟumi	poruḷ
1.	piratamā vipakti	karttā (viṉimutal)
2.	tuvitīya vipakti	karmam (ceyporuḷ)
3.	tirutīya vipakti	karttā, karaṇam (karuvi)
4.	caturttī vipakti	campiratāṉam (koṭi)
5.	pañcamī vipakti	apātāṉam (nīkkam)
6.	caṭṭī (ṣa ṣ ṭī)vipakti	campantam (toṭarpu)
7.	captamī vipakti	atikaraṇam (iṭam)
8.	campōtaṉa vipakti	campōtaṉam (vi)
teṉmoḻi vaṭamoḻikaḷiṉ muṉmi piṉmiyiyum mutaṉmi vaḻimiyiyum aṟiyāmiyāl, tamiḻilakkaṇam camaṟkiruta vilak kaṇattip piṉpaṟṟiyateṉap piṟaḻak kūṟiṉār kālṭuvel kaṇkāṇiyar eṉka. teṉmoḻiyilakkaṇam kumari nāṭṭil tōṉṟiyatu ki.mu. pattāyiram āṇṭukku muṉ eṉṟum, ataṉip piṉpaṟṟi vaṭamoḻiyilakkaṇam tōṉṟiyatu ki.mu. īrāyiram āṇṭukku muṉ eṉṟum aṟital vēṇṭum.
(6) vēṟṟumi, uṇmiyil ēḻēyāyiṉum peyariṉ eṇ nilikaḷuḷ perumpālāṉa vēṟṟumip paṭṭaviyātalālum, iyalpāṉa peyarum piṟavaṟṟōṭu oppu nōkka vēṟṟumippaṭulāṉum, vēṟṟumi eṭṭeṉak kūṟappaṭṭaṉa.
oru poruḷukku iṭumpeyar ataṉi eḻuvāyāka āḷutaṟkē yātaliṉ iyalpāṉa peyari mutal vēṟṟumiyākak koḷvatē moḻiyiṉ iyaṟki niliyām itaṟku māṟāka mutal vēṟṟumikkum tiripeyarkoḷvatu tiripuṭimoḻiyiṉ iyalpeṉka.

paṇṇu ṟatterin tāyntavip pacuntamiḻē ṉi
maṇṇi ṭiccila ilakkaṇa varampilā moḻipōl

pa-146-152. (1956-ākacṭu, cepṭampar.)
11
āytam
āyta eḻuttuvakimi
`eḻutteṉappaṭum
akara mutala ṉakara viṟuvāy
muppaḵteṉpa
cārntuvaral marapiṉ mūṉṟalaṅ kaṭiyē'
`avitām
kuṟṟiya likaram kuṟṟiya lukaram
āytam eṉṟa 
muppāṟ puḷḷiyum eḻuttō raṉṉa'	(tol.)
eṉṉum tolkāppiya nūṟpākkaḷāl (nūṉmarapu, 1, 2) mutaleḻuttu muppateṉpatum, cārpeḻuttu mūṉṟeṉpatum, ivaṟṟuḷ muṉṉiyavē ciṟantaṉa veṉpatum peṟappaṭum.
``muṉṉiṉṟa cūttirattāṟ cārntu varaṉ marapiṉ mūṉṟalaṅkaṭiyē eḻutteṉappaṭupa muppaḵteṉpa eṉavē, cārntuvaraṉ marapiṉ mūṉṟumē ciṟantaṉa; ēṉiya muppatum avvāṟu ciṟantilaveṉavum poruṭantu niṟṟaliṉ, ataṉi vilakkic ciṟanta muppatu eḻuttōṭu iviyum oppa vaḻaṅku meṉṟaṟku, eḻuttōraṉṉa eṉṟār'' eṉṟu nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kūṟutal kāṇka.
iṉi, ``ip peyarkaḷē peyar. immuṟiyē muṟi tokiyum mūṉṟē'' eṉṟu nacciṉārkkiṉiyar tēṟṟēkāramiṭṭuk kūṟuvatālum, naṉṉūlār cārpeṉak kūṟiya pattu eḻuttukkaḷuḷ ēṉiya vēḻum tolkāppiyarkkum nacciṉārkkiṉiyarkkum naṉṉūlār koḷki yiruntatilli yeṉpatu teḷivām.
āytappeyarum oliyum
tolkāppiyar āytattik kuṟikkumpōtē muppāṟpuḷḷi eṉa ataṟku maṟṟumoru peyar koṭuttār. piṟkālat tāciriyar vēṟuñ cila peyariṭṭu vaḻaṅkiṉar. ``āytam eṉpatu aḵkēṉam. aḵkēṉameṉiṉum āytameṉiṉum taṉinili yeṉiṉum puḷḷi yeṉiṉum oṟṟeṉiṉum okkum. eṉṉi?
`aḵkēṉa māytan taṉinili puḷḷi
yoṟṟip pāla vintu mitaṟkē'
eṉṟār mayēccurar eṉṟu kārikiyuriyāciriyarāṉa kuṇacākararum, ``āytam eṉiṉum, akkēṉam eṉiṉum, taṉinili eṉiṉum, puḷḷi eṉiṉum, oṟṟu eṉiṉum okkum. eṉṉi?
`akkēṉa māytan taṉinili puḷḷi
yoṟṟip pāla vintu mitaṟkē'
eṉṟār avinayaṉār'' eṉṟu yāpparuṅ kalaviruttikārarum kūṟuvatu kāṇka.
avinaya nūṟpāvil akkēṉameṉpatu ēṭṭup piḻiyāyiruttal vēṇṭum. paḻiya tiṇṇip paḷḷip pāṭamuṟiyil āytappeyar ataṉ oli vaṭivoṭṭi aḵkaṉṉā eṉa vaḻaṅkiṟṟu
āytavoli
ēṉiyiru cārpeḻuttup pōṉṟē, āytamum oru mutaleḻut tiṉ tiripeṉpatu, ``itaṟku (āytattiṟku) vaṭivu kūṟiṉār, ēṉiyoṟṟu kaḷ pōla uyirēṟātu ōci vikāramāy niṟpatoṉṟākaliṉ,'' eṉṉum nacciṉārkkiṉiyar uriyālum,
`cārntuvari ṉallatu tamakkiyal pilaveṉat 
tērntuveḷip paṭutta ēṉi mūṉṟum
tattañ cārpiṟ piṟappoṭu civaṇi
yotta kāṭciyiṟ ṟammiyal piyalum'	(piṟappiyal, 19)
eṉṉum tolkāppiya nūṟpāvālum, cārpeḻuttu eṉṉum peyarālum, aṟiyappaṭum. āyiṉ, evveḻuttiṉ tiripeṉiṉ, kakarattiṉ tiripām eṉka.
āytam toṉṟutoṭṭu melliya kakaramākavē olikkappaṭṭu varuvatālum, ēṉac cāriyi peṟṟakkāl aḵkēṉam eṉak kakarattō ṭiyintē vaḻaṅkuvatālum, paṇṭiyēṭ ṭariccuvaṭikaḷil ka - ḵ eṉṟu eḻutappeṟṟamiyālum uyirukkum meykkum iṭiyil vikkappaṭ ṭiruppiṉum kakaratti yaṭuttiruppatālum, pōppiyar tam tamiḻilak kaṇattil `The Guttural' eṉṟum, kālṭuvel tam oppiyalilakkaṇattil ``guttural `h' '' eṉṟum kuṟittiruppatālum, ceṉṉip palkalik kaḻakat tamiḻ akarātiyil ataṉ amippu muṟiyi viḷakkumiṭattu `The aspirate aytam' eṉṟu kuṟittiruppiṉum, varipeyarppil (Transliteration) k eṉṉuṅkuṟiyi āḷvatālum āytaveḻuttu kakarattiṉ tiripēyeṉat teṟṟeṉa vaṟika.
āriya vaṉmi cāṉṟa valleḻuttukkaḷ tamiḻukkiṉmiyiṉ, āytatti vaṭamoḻi hakaram (h) pōṉṟa mūccoliyākak koḷvatu muṟṟum tavaṟām. iṉi vaṭamoḻi vicarkkam (.) pōṉṟa oliyeṉak koḷḷiṉ, oliyaḷavil vicarkkam āytatti yottiruppiṉum, atil akarak kūṟum kalantiruppatāl, atuvum poruntāteṉka. āytam evvakiyiṉum uyirēṟappeṟā eḻuttām ``kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉa rāytap puḷḷi'' eṉṉum nūṟpā vuriyil, ``āytamāṉa puḷḷi, `āytappuḷḷi' eṉṟār, itaṉiyum oṟṟiṉpāṟ cārttutaṟ keṉka. oṟṟēl uyirēṟap peṟal vēṇṭumeṉiṉ, cārpeḻuttātaliṉ uyirēṟap peṟāteṉak koḷka'' eṉa mayilinātar urittiruppatik kāṇka.
``āytam (``aṟṟālaḷavaṟin tuṇkavaḵtuṭampu'' mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil uyirpōla alaku peṟṟum, ``tōṉṟiṟ pukaḻoṭu tōṉṟuka vaḵtilār'' mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil meypōla alaku peṟāmalum uyirmeykaḷi (orupuṭiyottu) uyirumākāmal meyyumākāmal (alipōlat) taṉiniṟṟalāl, `taṉinili' eṉappaṭṭatu'' eṉṟār naṉṉūṟ kāṇṭiki yurikārarākiya caṭakōparāmāṉucāccāriyār. ``aṟṟālaḷavaṟin tuṇka vaḵtuṭampu'' eṉṉum pāṭattiṟku āytam alaku peṟal vēṇṭāmi yāṉum, avvāccāriyārē ``uyirum meyyum tammiṟ kalattal pōla āytam ōreḻuttōṭuṅ kalattaliṉṟit taṉi niṟṟalāl taṉinili yeṉappaṭṭa teṉṟalu moṉṟu,'' eṉak kūṟiyiruttalāṉum avvuri poruntāteṉka. āytam kakarattiṉpāṟpaṭṭa ora nuṇṇiya voli yāyiruttal paṟṟiyē appeyar peṟṟateṉ ṟaṟika.
`ōytal āytal niḻattal cāay
āvayiṉ nāṉkum uḷḷataṉ nuṇukkam' 	(uriyiyal, 32)
eṉpatu tolkāppiyam. āytap peyarkkāraṇaṅ kūṟumiṭattu, ``that which is subtle, minute'' eṉṟār kālṭuvelum.
ceṉṉip palkalik kaḻakat tamiḻakarāti, tāṉ koṇṭa kuṟikkōṭkoppa, tamiḻc coṟkaḷit tamiḻvaḻi ārāyātu, āytam eṉṉum teṉcollic cārntirukki yeṉṟu poruḷpaṭum āciritam eṉṉum vaṭacoṟṟiripā yirukkalāmeṉak karuti mayaṅkukiṉṟatu. kuṟṟiyalukaram kuṟṟiyalikaram āytam eṉac cārpeḻuttu mūṉṟāyirukka, avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟiṟku maṭṭum cārntirukkip poruṭcol eṅṅaṉam peyarā yamintiruttal kūṭum? vaṭamoḻiyit tamiḻukkaṭippaṭiyāy vittu, oli muṟic colliyal (Sound Etymology) paṟṟit teṉcoṟkaḷ palavaṟṟi vaṭacolleṉak kūṟuvatu, vaṭamoḻiyāḷarkkut toṉṟu toṭṭa vaḻakkamāyiruntu varukiṉṟatu.
iṉi, āytam eṉṉum teṉcolli āyutam eṉṉum vaṭacol loṭu mayakki, aṭuppukkūṭṭum kēṭakam mūvilic cūlam ākiya paṭikkalamum pōl varivaṭivu koṇṭiruppatāl āytam eṉap peyar peṟṟatu eṉak kāraṇaṅkāṭṭuvārum uḷar. āytam eṉṉum col ōriṭattum āyutam eṉa eḻutap paṭāmiyiyum, āyutam eṉṉum collum āytam eṉa eḻutappaṭāmiyiyum, nōkkuka. āriyar nāvalan tēyattiṟku varumuṉṉarē, āytaveḻuttum ataṉ peyarum kumarināṭṭil tōṉṟiviṭṭaṉa eṉṉum uṇmiyiyum aṟintu koḷka.
iṉi, yāpparuṅkalavirutti yurikārar ``uyirē meyyē'' eṉṉum nūṟpāvuriyil,
``cārpiṟ ṟōṉṟum taṉmiya'' eṉṟu mikuttuc colliya vataṉāl, akarattoṭu yakaravoṟṟu vantum āytam vantum aikārattiṉ payattavāy niṟkum......................................
`āytamum yavvu mavvoṭu variṉē
aiyeṉeḻuttoṭu meypeṟat tōṉṟum'
eṉṟār avinayaṉār.
``varalāṟu...................................f~R, kaḵtam, kaḵcamedakarattoLāytamvaªtukiR,kita«,kirmvṉaikāratâṉpayattavāyiṉavāṟu............................'' eṉa urittiruppatu, āytattippoṟuttamaṭṭišuttikku¥poruntuvataṉṟu. kaḵcu mutaliya mūṉṟum kicu mutaliya mūṉṟākat tirintirukkalāmēyaṉṟi, āytam yakaramey yottolit tiruttal muṭiyātu. orukāl, kaḷ eṉpatu kaḵcu mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ aṭiyākaliṉ, atu kay eṉat tirintirukkalām. oppunōkka : toḷ - toy, poḷ - poy. 
āytap piṟappu
``cārntuvari ṉallatu'' eṉṉum cārpeḻuttup piṟappu nūṟpāviṉ āṟṟoḻukkik koṇṭukūṭṭākac citittu, irukūṟākki, ``cilavḻuttukaḷicrrntuntāṉṟiṉallatujmakkeṉatntāṉṟutaṟkuXriyalpilaveṉṟu,Mrāyntubvaḷippaṭukkappaṭṭavḻuttukaḷjmmuṭiyaãṟappiyalpu_ṉṟiṉiyuṅTṟuṅkāl,jttamakkucriyarrpākiyabmaykaḷatuáṟappupãṟappiṭattēãṟattalōṭubpāruntieṭakkum''vṉṟum,``oḻintaMytanjmakkupbpāruntiṉabnañcutḷiyāṟãṟakkum''vṉṟumcrittaṉarecciṉārkkiṉiyar.ïª nūṟpā muḻuvatiyum orē kūṟṟākak koṇṭu, `cila eḻuttu kaḷic cārntuvariṉallatu tamakkeṉat taṉiyiyalpillātaṉaveṉa ārāyntu veḷippaṭuttappaṭṭa (kuṟṟiyalukaramum kuṟṟiyalikaramum āytamumākiya) maṟṟa mūṉṟu eḻuttukaḷum, tattam mutaleḻuttukaḷiṉ piṟappoṭuporunti avaṟṟōṭorupuṭi yotta tōṟṟattiṉavāyt tattam ciṟapput taṉmiyuṭaṉ naṭakkum' eṉṟurippatē poruttamām.
``iṉi āytan talivaḷiyāṉum miṭaṟṟuvaḷiyāṉum piṟakku meṉpārum uḷar'' eṉa nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kūṟiyiruppatu kavaṉikkattakkatu.
āytac cāriyi
`kāramuṅ karamuṅ kāṉoṭu civaṇi
nērat tōṉṟum eḻuttiṉ cāriyi'	(puṇariyal, 32)
eṉṉum eḻuttuccāriyi nūṟpāvil āytaveḻuttiṟkuriya cāriyiyi āciriyar kūṟāviṭiṉum, ``nērattōṉṟum eṉavē nērattōṉṟātaṉavum uḷavāyiṉa. avi āṉam, ēṉam, ōṉam eṉka. ivi citinta vaḻakkēṉuṅ kaṭiyalākāvāyiṉa'' eṉṟu uriyāciriyar (nacciṉārkkiṉiyar) kūṟiyiruppatu kavaṉikkattakkatu. tolkāppiya nūliṟ kūṟappaṭāta cila ilakkaṇaṅkaḷ uriyāciriyarāṟ koḷḷappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa avaṟṟuḷ āytaveḻuttuc cāriyiyum oṉṟu. itu uriyiṟ kōṭal eṉṉum uttipaṟṟiyatu.
āytac cārpu
āytam, kuṟilukku muṉṉum valliṉavuyirmeykkup piṉṉumāka avviraṇṭiṟkumiṭiyil varuvatē iyalpām.
`kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉa rāytap puḷḷi
uyiroṭu puṇarntaval lāṟaṉ micittē	(moḻimarapu, 5)
eṉṟār tolkāppiyaṉār.
`āytan tāṉē kuṟiyataṉ kīḻttāy
valiyataṉ mēlvan tiyalu meṉpa'
eṉṟār kiyaṉār.
ivvitikku, eḵku, kaḵcu, kaḵṭu, kaḵtu, kaḵpu, kaḵṟu, aḵtu iḵtu, uḵtu eṉa nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum; aḵkam, veḵkā, eḵku, kaḵcu, kaḵṭu, kaḵtu, kaḵpu, kaḵṟu eṉa yāpparuṅkala viruttiyuriyāciriyarum; eḵku, kaḵcu, kaḵṭu, kaḵtu, kaḵpu, kaḵṟu eṉak kuṇacākararum; eḵku, kaḵcu, kaḵṭu, kaḵtu, kaḵpu, kaḵṟu eṉa mayili nātarum eṭuttukkāṭṭiṉar ivi taṉic coṟkaḷ. aḵṟiṇi, muḵṭītu, aḵkaṭiya eṉpaṉa toṭarccoṟkaḷ.
`vilaḵḵki vīṅkiru ḷōṭṭumē māta
rilaḵḵku mutti ṉiṉam'
eṉa ivvāṟu kuṟiliṇik kīḻum āytam varumālōveṉiṉ, aṉṉa iyalpākavē āytamāy niṟpaṉavalla. ōci niṟittaṟporuṭṭu oṟṟiṉvaḻi oṟṟākkutaliṉ, īṇṭu āytamum oṟṟāy varumeṉak koḷka'' eṉṟu mayilinātar kūṟiyiruppatu iṅkuk kavaṉikkattakkatu.
``laḷaḵkāṉ'' (nūṉmarapu, 24) ``ṇaṉaḵkāṉ'' (kṣa 26) eṉat tolkāppiyattil vantiruppavi ummit toṭarkaḷātaliṉ, kuṟiliṇikkīḻvantaṉa vākā. āyiṉ, ``iḷaḵkumē'' eṉac cintāmaṇiyil (149) vantiruppatu vitikku māṟātaliṉ, itaṉiyum oṟṟil vaḻiyoṟ ṟeṉavē koḷḷal vēṇṭum.
āytam tōṉṟum vaki
lakarattiripu, vakarattiripu, ḷakarattiripu, oṟṟil vaḻiyoṟṟu, cāriyip puṇarppu eṉa āytam tōṉṟum vaki aintām.
`takaram varuvaḻi yāyta niliyalum
pukariṉ ṟeṉmaṉār pulami yōrē'	(puḷḷimayaṅkiyal, 74)
eṉpatu lakarattiripu. kaḵṟītu, kaṟṟītu eṉpaṉa eṭuttukkāṭṭu. ivi uṟaḻcci. aḵṟiṇi paḵṟuḷi eṉpaṉa uṟaḻcciyillātaṉa.
`vēṟṟumi yalvaḻi yāyta mākum'	(pu.ma.84)
eṉpatu vakarattiripu. ``aḵkaṭiya, iḵkaṭiya, uḵkaṭiya, ciṟiya, tīya, periya'' eṉpaṉa eṭuttukkāṭṭu.
`āyta niliyalum varinili yiṉṟē
takaram varūuṅ kāli yāṉa'	(pu. ma. 104)
eṉpatu ḷakarattiripu. muḵṭītu, muṭṭītu eṉpaṉa eṭuttukkāṭṭu. ivi uṟaḻcci.
oṟṟil vaḻiyoṟṟu muṉṉark kāṭṭappeṟṟatu.
aḵkāṉ, maḵkāṉ, vaḵkāṉ eṉpaṉa cāriyip puṇarppu. 
`aḵtu' eṉpataṉ kuṟukkamē `atu' eṉat teritaliṉ, `ceyvaḵtu' eṉpatu `ceyvatu' eṉpataṉ virittal tiripeṉak koḷvatu cariyaṉṟu.
vakarattiripum cāriyip puṇarppum ottiyalamivu (Assimilation) eṉṉum neṟimuṟi paṟṟiyaṉa.
āytam varum taṉiccoṟkaḷ
āytac cārpiyum āytan tōṉṟum vakiyiyum tuṇik koṇṭu āytam varum taṉiccoṟkaḷi āyntu pārppiṉ, avaṟṟuṭ perumpālāṉa lakara ḷakarat tiripākavē pōtarum. it tiripu kumarināṭṭiṉ oru maruṅku tōṉṟiyirukkalām.
