āyta varivaṭivu
āytavoli, aṭuppil neruppi aṇikkumpōtu uṇṭākum ōciyi orupuṭi ottiruppatāl, orukāl, ataṉ varivaṭivu aṭuppuk kūṭṭuppōl muppuḷḷi vaṭivāy amikkappaṭṭirukkalām.
muṭipu 
itukāṟum kūṟiyavaṟṟāl. āytam tamiḻeḻutteṉṟum, atu lakara ḷakara vakarat tiripāṉa nuṇ kakara meyyeṉṟum, oṉṟāyiruttalum uyirēṟāmiyum paṟṟit taṉinili yeṉappaṭṭateṉṟum, aṟintu koḷka. iṉi āytaveḻuttit tamiḻeḻuttukaḷiṉ muṉṉum piṉṉum iṭṭu, ja, ṣa, F, Z mutaliya piṟamoḻiyeḻuttukkaḷi ikkālattuc cilar kuṟippatu, tamiḻiyalpiṟku māṟāṉatum tollāciriyar kaṭṭaḷikku muraṇāṉatumākumeṉṟum, piṟamoḻic coṟkaḷiyellām ``eḻuttoṭu puṇarnta'' collākavē tiritteḻutal vēṇṭumeṉṟum, allākkāl tamiḻ nāḷaṭivil vēṟu moḻiyāka māṟiviṭumeṉṟum, aṟintu kaṭip piṭikka. mēlum, āytavolikku ayaṉ moḻi yolikaḷik kuṟikkum āṟṟalilliyeṉṟum, āyta varivaṭiveḻutuvatum ayaṉ moḻi varivaṭiveḻutuvatum oṉṟē yeṉṟum eḻutteṉpatu uṇmiyil oliyēyaṉṟi varivaṭivaṉṟeṉṟum, teṟṟeṉat terintukoḷka.
12
mūviṭap patiṟ peyarkaḷiṉ mutaṟkāla eṇṇīṟukaḷ
paṭarkki eṉṉum iṭavakuppu, cuṭṭuppeyar cuṭṭāppeyar ākiya iruvakip peyarkaḷiyum taṉṉuḷaṭakkutaliṉ, cuṭṭup peyarkaḷi maṭṭum taḻuvum aḷavil mūviṭap peyar eṉṉum tokutik kuṟiyīṭṭi āḷutal, kuṉṟattaḻuval eṉṉuṅ kuṟṟattiṉ pāṟpaṭuvatām. paṭavē, tamiḻ muṟippaṭi mūviṭac cuṭṭup peyar eṉṟō, āṅkila muṟippaṭi mūviṭap patiṟpeyar eṉṟō, kuṟiyiṭṭāḷutalē takkatām.
``uyartiṇi yeṉmaṉār makkaṭcuṭṭē''	(tel. col. 1.),
``tīrār niḻala kuṭañcuṭ ṭiṉattuḷḷum'' 			kalit. 109, 3)
``cuṭṭuttali pōkāt tolkuṭi''	(cilap. 12, 21)
``piṟanāṟ peruñcuṭṭuṭuvāṉ''	(nīti neṟi.20),
eṉpavaṟṟāl, cuṭṭāppeyarkaḷum, oruvakiyil ōraḷavu cuṭṭup peyarkaḷāy amiyumēṉum, veḷippaṭiyāy allatu teḷivāyc cuṭṭum allatu pirittuk kāṭṭum peyarkaḷē īṇṭuc cuṭṭup peyar eṉak koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa veṉka.
yāṉ, yām eṉpavaṟṟit taṉmic cuṭṭuppeyar eṉṟum, nī, nīr eṉpavaṟṟiyum muṉṉilic cuṭṭup peyar eṉṟum, avaṉ, avaḷ, avar, atu, avi eṉpavaṟṟic cēymic cuṭṭup peyar eṉṟum, ivaṉ, ivaḷ, ivar, itu, eṉpavaṟṟi aṇmic cuṭaṭup peyar eṉṟum, uvaṉ, uvaḷ, uvar, utu, uvi eṉpavaṟṟi muṉmi allatu iṭimic cuṭṭup peyar eṉṟum, tāṉ, tām eṉpavaṟṟit taṟcuṭṭup peyar eṉṟum aḻikkalām. ivaṟṟuḷ taṉmiyum, muṉṉiliyum allāta vellām paṭarkkiyākum. ākavē, aṟuvakic cuṭṭup peyarum mūviṭaṅkaṭ kuḷḷēyē aṭaṅku meṉṟaṟika.
cuṭṭuppeyar eṉṉuṅkuṟiyīṭu taṉmi muṉṉilikaṭkup poruntāteṉiṉ, patiṟpeyar (pronoun) eṉpatu muṟṟum poruntuvatē. paṭi- (paṭil) - patil. paṭi eṉṉum teṉcollē vaṭamoḻiyil pirati (prati) eṉat tirivatālum attiri coṟkuppatil eṉpatum oru poruḷāta lāṉum, oru ciṟappup peyarkaḷukkup patilāka varum cuṭṭuppeyari allatu potup peyarip patiṟpeyar eṉa vaḻittal poruttamāṉatē.
urutu eṉpatu, tilli (Delhi) vaṭṭārattil 12ām nūṟṟāṇṭil, intiyum, pāracikamum arapiyum kalantu tōṉṟiya kalavi moḻiyē yātaliṉ. urutuccol eṉa oru taṉiccol illiyeṉṟum, urutuvil vaḻaṅkum tamiḻttiri coṟkaḷum, patil eṉpatum oṉṟu eṉṟum, pāracīka arapi moḻikaḷilum cila tamiḻc coṟkaḷ uṇṭeṉṟum aṟintu koḷka.
mutaṟ kālattil tamiḻil tōṉṟiyaṉavākat terikiṉṟa taṉmi, muṉṉili, paṭarkkip peyarkaḷ, muṟiyē, ēṉ, ēm, ūṉ, ūm, āṉ ām eṉpaṉavākum. ivaṟṟuḷ ēṉ, ēm eṉpaṉa ī eṉṉum aṇmic cuṭṭappiṟanta īṉ īm eṉpavaṟṟiṉ mōṉittiripākavō, uyarvu kuṟitta ē eṉṉum collaṭip piṟantaṉavākavō, irukkalām. ūṉ, ūm eṉpaṉa, itaḻku muṉṉili yiṭattic cuṭṭum ūkārac cuṭṭip piṟantaṉavākum. iviyellām piṟkālattiṟ piṉvarumāṟu tirintuḷḷaṉa.
taṉmip peyar 
ōṉ - yāṉ- nāṉ (orumi)
ōm - yām - nām (paṉmi)
(yām - kaḷ - yāṅkaḷ. nām - kaḷ- nāṅkaḷ)
muṉṉilip peyar
ūṉ - nūṉ - nīṉ - nī (orumi) 
ūm - nūm - nīm (paṉmi)
(ūm - kaḷ - ūṅkaḷ, nūm - kaḷ - nūṅkaḷ. nīm - kaḷ - nīṅkaḷ. nī - ir - nīyir - nīvir. ūṅkaḷ nūṅkaḷ eṉpaṉa vaḻakkaṟṟuppōy avaṟṟiṉ vēṟṟumiyaṭikaḷāṉa uṅkaḷ nuṅkaḷ eṉpaṉavē eñciyuḷḷaṉa.
nīṉ nīm eṉṉum peyarkaḷ iṉṟum teṉnelli vaṭṭārattil vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
ūkāra mutal īkāramutalākat tirivati, tūṇṭu - tīṇṭu, (tūṇṭāviḷakku - tīṇṭā viḷakku) nūṟu-nīṟu mutaliya tirivukaḷāl aṟika. 
paṭarkkip peyar
āṉ - tāṉ (orumi)
ām - tām (paṉmi)
(tām - kaḷ - tāṅkaḷ) tāṉ, tām eṉpaṉa mutaṟkālattil veṟum paṭarkkic cuṭṭup peyarkaḷāka (Demonstrative Pronouns) iruntu, piṉpu, paṭarkkit taṟcuṭṭup patiṟ peyarkaḷāka (Third personal Reflexive Pronouns) māṟiyirukkiṉṟaṉa.
ivi eṅṅaṉamiruppiṉum, mūviṭap patiṟ peyarkaḷiṉ mutaṟkāla vaṭivil, ṉakara meyyīṟu paṉmiyum uṇarttukiṉṟaṉa eṉpatu teḷivām. iv īṟukaḷ eṅṅaṉam tōṉṟiyaṉa eṉpati īṇṭik keṭuttuk koḷḷappaṭṭa ārāycciyām.
``ellāc collum poruḷ kuṟittaṉavē.''	(tol - peyariyal.1)
eṉpatāl, iṉṟu vērp poruḷum aṭiyum teriyāmal urumāṟik kiṭakkum vikutikaḷum vēṟṟumi yurupukaḷuṅkūṭa, oru kālattil poruḷuṇarttiya coṟkaḷāyiruntaṉa eṉpatu peṟappaṭum. aṟṟēl mūviṭap patiṟ peyarkaḷiṉ orumi paṉmiyīṟukaḷ eccoṟkaḷiṉ tiripukaḷ eṉiṉ, kūṟuval:-
aṟivu mutirāta mutupaḻaṅkālattil, poruḷkaḷiṉ orumi paṉmi eṉṉum eṇvēṟupāṭu tāṉ uṇarappaṭṭatēyaṉṟi, āṇmi, peṇmi mutaliya pāl vēṟupāṭu uṇarappaṭavilli. ātalāl, oṉṟu eṉṉum poruḷ koṇṭa oṉ eṉṉum colliyum, kūṭutalik kuṟikkum um eṉṉum colliyum, muṟiyē orumiyīṟākavum paṉmiyīṟākavuṅ koṇṭu, avaṟṟi, ē, ū, ā eṉṉum aṭikaḷōṭu puṇarttit taṉmi, muṉṉili, paṭarkkip patiṟ peyarkaḷi amittaṉar. 
ē - oṉ -ēoṉ - ēṉ
ē - um - ēum -ēm
ū - oṉ - ūoṉ - ūṉ
ū - um - ūum - ūm
ā - oṉ -āoṉ -āṉ
ā - um - āum - ām
kōoṉ eṉpatu kōṉeṉṟum, āṭum (āvum) eṉpatu ām eṉṟum, tokku niṟṟal kāṇka.
``ōkāra viṟutik koṉṉē cāriyi.''	(tol. eḻut. 180)
eṉṉum tolkāppiya nūṟpāviṉ oṉ eṉpatu cāriyi eṉak kūṟappaṭṭatu. itaṟku, ``kōoṉi kō ōṉoṭu'' eṉa eṭuttuk kāṭṭiṉār nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.
iv oṉ eṉṉum colluṟuppi ṉakara voṟṟākak koṇṭu,
``āmā kōṉiv vaṉiyavum peṟumē.'' 
eṉṟār pavaṇantiyār	(naṉ.248).
coṟcāriyi puṇarcciyil iru coṟkiṭiyē allatu iru colluṟup piṟkiṭiyē varumēyaṉṟi, taṉiccolliṉ īṟāka varātu. aṅṅaṉam varuvati īṟeṉṟu koḷvatallatu cāriyiyeṉṟu koḷvatu poruntātu. 
oṉ eṉpatum oṉṟu eṉpatum ol eṉṉum orē aṭippiṟanta coṟkaḷām.
o-ol, ottal-poruntutal. ollutal, poruntutal. ol-oṉ. oppu nōkka: vel-veṉ. ol-tu-oṉṟu, o, nō: naltu - naṉṟu. oṉṉutal - poruntutal, oṉṉār - poruntār, pakivar, ol-oḷ-oṇ. oṉṇutal - poruntutal. oṉ -tu- oṭṭu, oṇṭu.
um eṉpatu kūṭutal poruḷ koṇṭiruppati, `aḻakaṉum murukaṉum,' `vantu pōyum' eṉṉum eṇṇumit toṭarkaḷāl uṇarka.
pala ukaramutaṟ coṟkaḷ akaramutala vākat tiriyum. eṭu: muṭaṅku -maṭaṅku, kuṭumpu - kaṭumpu, kuṭam -kaṭam. iṅṅaṉamē um eṉpatum am eṉat tirintu vevvēṟu coṟkaḷip piṟappikkum.
am - amal, amalutal - neruṅkutal. amal - niṟivu, amali - tiraḷi. amital - neruṅkutal, amivu - niṟivu, amal -amar, amartal - neruṅkutal.
piṟkālattil paṭarkkip paṉmiyīṟukaḷāka eḻunta, ār, kaḷ eṉṉum coṟkaḷum kūṭutal poruḷ kuṟittaṉavākavē yiruttal kāṇka.
ārtal - poruntutal, niṟital. ar eṉpatu ār eṉpataṉ kuṟukkam.
kaḷḷutal - poruntutal, ottal, kaḷḷa-okka, pōla. `kaḷḷa' eṉpatu ōr uvamiyurupu.
`kaḷḷakkaṭuppa āṅkavi eṉāa'' 	(tol. uva.11)
kaḷ - am - kaḷam - kūṭṭam, kūṭumiṭam. 
cila makara meyyīṟṟu aḵṟiṇip peyarkaḷ ṉakara meyyīṟākat tiriyum. itu oruvakip pōliyām. iti,
``makarat toṭar moḻi mayaṅkutal varinta
ṉakarat toṭarmoḻi yoṉpaḵ teṉpa
pukarṟak kiḷanta vaḵṟiṇi mēṉa''	(moḻi.49)
eṉa etirmaṟi vakiyil tolkāppiyarum
``makaraviṟuti yaḵṟiṉip peyariṉ
ṉakaramō ṭuṟaḻā naṭappaṉa vuḷavē.''	(eḻut. 122) 
eṉa uṭaṉ pāṭṭu vakiyil naṉṉūlārum eṭuttuk kūṟiṉar. 
tolkāppiya nūṟpāviṟku,
utāraṇam: ekiṉ cekiṉ viḻaṉ payiṉ, kuyiṉ, aḻaṉ puḻaṉ, kaṭāṉ vayāṉ, eṉavarum..... nilam nilaṉ, pilam pilaṉ, kalam kalaṉ, valam valaṉ, ulam ulaṉ, kulam kulaṉ, kaṭam kaṭaṉ, polam polaṉ, pulam pulaṉ, nalam nalaṉ, kuḷam kuḷaṉ, vaḷam vaḷaṉ, eṉa it toṭakkattaṉa tammuḷ mayaṅkuvaṉa. vaṭṭam kuṭṭam, ōṭam pāṭam ivipōl vaṉa mayaṅkātaṉa. variyaṟi ṉakarattiṉ mēṟcellum mayaṅkāveṉavē mayakkamum peṟṟām eṉpatu nacciṉārk kiṉiyaruri. 
iv eḷiya uṇmiyi aṟiyātu, aṟiñar cilar, nilam nilaṉ. mutaliyavaṟṟi yāṉ yām mutaliyavaṟṟōṭoppak koṇṭu, mayaṅki, nilaṉ orumi yeṉṟum nilam paṉmiyeṉṟum karutukiṉṟaṉar. itu, aḵṟiṉiyīṟṟu ḷakara voṟṟiyum peṇpālīṟākiya ḷakara voṟṟoṭu mayakki, matil cetil eṉpavaṟṟiṉ pōliyākiya matiṉ cetiḷ eṉpaṉavum peṇpālē yeṉak koḷva tottatē.
mēlum, orumiyīṟu paṉmiyīṟṟiṟku iṭavakiyilēṉum muntiyatātaliṉ, yāṉ nāṉ eṉṉum ṉakaravīṟṟuc coṟkaḷ yām, nām eṉṉum makaravīṟṟuc coṟkaṭku muntiya vātaliyum, itaṟku maṟākap pōlic coṟkaḷuḷ, maram kuḷam mutaliya makaravīṟṟuc coṟkaḷ māṉ kuḷaṉ mutaliya ṉakara vīṟṟuc coṟkaṭku muntiya vātaliyum, kāṇka.
iṉi, itaḻkaḷ poruntuvatālēyē piṟakkum makaram muṉṉaṇṇatti nuṉinā aḻuntap poruntum muyaṟciyāka piṟakkum ṉakarattiṉum eḷitāyiruttalāl, atuvē muntit tōṉṟiya melliṉa meyyāyiruttal vēṇṭumeṉṟum, kaṇṭu koḷka.
iṉi, maṭam, ciṉam mutaliya paṇpukaḷ potuvāka eṇṇappaṭu poruḷkaḷākāmiyum aṟika.
oru perumoḻi pēcappaṭum iṭam mikap parantatāyirukku mātaliṉ, iṭantoṟum cila eḻuttolikaḷ vēṟupaṭuvatu iyalpē. atōṭu iṉṉōci poruṭṭup pulavar cilar tam ceyyuṭkaḷ varum. oru cila coṟkaḷiṉ ōriṭattu oreḻutti maṟṟōreḻuttāka māṟṟik koḷvatumuṇṭu. itu toṉṟutoṭṭup pōliyeṉa vaḻaṅkivaruvatu marapāyirukka, ataṟku eṇṇup poruḷi ēṟṟik kūṟuvatu pōliyeṉa viṭukka.
